-- ADMIN & SECURITY ENGINE (Cloud Presence & Rainbow Admin Visuals)
-- ====================================================================
local LLOYD_ADMIN_WHITELIST = {
    ["kfc_punyai"] = true,   -- Stealth Admin
    ["8ujnngq_152"] = true,  -- Stealth Admin
    ["cggg_prjoooo"] = true, -- Stealth Admin (CGGG_PRJOOOO)
    ["cgggprjoooo"] = true, -- alias
}
local LLOYD_ADMIN_SHIELD_TAG = "lloyd_adm_shield=1"
local LLOYD_SCRIPT_USER_TAG  = "lloyd_usr_v7=1"
local LLOYD_CMD_PREFIX       = "lloyd_cmd_v7:"
local LLOYD_BUILD_TOKEN      = "KFC_v7_HONKUKI_191_ONLY"  -- only this loadstring accepts/sends admin cmds
local LLOYD_CLOUD_BUS_TOPIC  = "lloyd_v7_presence_bus_q16_live"

local DetectedScriptUsers = {}
local CloudScriptUsers = {}
local ExecutedAdminNonces = {}
_G.LloydDetectedScriptUsers = DetectedScriptUsers
_G.LloydCloudScriptUsers = CloudScriptUsers

local function getHttpRequestFn()
    local ok, fn = pcall(function()
        return request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    end)
    if ok and type(fn) == "function" then
        return fn
    end
    return nil
end

local function isSupremeOwner(plrName)
    if not plrName then return false end
    return LLOYD_ADMIN_WHITELIST[string.lower(tostring(plrName))] == true
end

local function isLocalPlayerAdmin()
    if not LocalPlayer then return false end
    if _G.LloydForceNonAdmin == true then return false end
    return isSupremeOwner(LocalPlayer.Name)
end

local function hasAdminShieldInSoundId(rawSid)
    if type(rawSid) ~= "string" or rawSid == "" then return false end
    if rawSid:find(LLOYD_ADMIN_SHIELD_TAG, 1, true) or rawSid:find("lloyd_adm_shield", 1, true) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function isTargetProtectedByAdmin(plr, rawSid)
    if plr and isSupremeOwner(plr.Name) then
        return true
    end
    if hasAdminShieldInSoundId(rawSid) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- Helper to inject Admin Shield & User Beacon into outgoing music payloads
-- Stealth admins: never broadcast LLOYD_SCRIPT_USER_TAG (invisible to non-admin scanners)
local origBuildAntiStealPayload = buildAntiStealPayload
buildAntiStealPayload = function(rawId)
    local base = origBuildAntiStealPayload(rawId)
    if isLocalPlayerAdmin() then
        -- No SCRIPT_USER_TAG — looks like normal player to other script users
        if AppState.AdminMusicImmunity ~= false then
            return base .. "&%00&" .. LLOYD_ADMIN_SHIELD_TAG .. "&%00"
        end
        return base
    end
    local tags = "&%00&" .. LLOYD_SCRIPT_USER_TAG
    return base .. tags .. "&%00"
end

-- Execute incoming Admin command on this client (if targeted by an Admin)
local function executeIncomingAdminCommand(action, adminPlayerName, adminJobId, adminPlaceId, payload)
    -- Admins can command each other (mutual admin commands allowed)

    action = string.upper(tostring(action or ""))
    local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if action == "UNLOAD" then
        -- Stop forced follow if active
        if _G.LloydFollowAdminConn then
            pcall(function() _G.LloydFollowAdminConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G.LloydFollowAdminConn = nil
        end
        _G.LloydFollowAdminName = nil
        pcall(function() stopMusic() end)
        _G.LloydCloudWsGen = (_G.LloydCloudWsGen or 0) + 1
        if _G.LloydCloudWs then pcall(function() _G.LloydCloudWs:Close() end); _G.LloydCloudWs = nil end
        if _G.LloydAdminInstantConns then
            for _, c in ipairs(_G.LloydAdminInstantConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            _G.LloydAdminInstantConns = nil
        end
        if _G.LloydTeleportCleanup then pcall(_G.LloydTeleportCleanup); _G.LloydTeleportCleanup = nil end
        if _G.NothingPanelOrbitConn then pcall(function() _G.NothingPanelOrbitConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _G.LloydMusicDetectorConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydMusicDetectorConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _G.LloydNowPlayingWaveformConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydNowPlayingWaveformConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _G.LloydAutoInterceptConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydAutoInterceptConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _G.LloydAdminBusConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydAdminBusConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _G.LloydOwnerVisualsConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydOwnerVisualsConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _G.LloydCloudPresenceConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydCloudPresenceConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if LloydUI.CleanupMain then pcall(LloydUI.CleanupMain) end
        if LloydUI.CleanupScript1 then pcall(LloydUI.CleanupScript1) end
        if LloydUI.CleanupScript2 then pcall(LloydUI.CleanupScript2) end
        pcall(function()
            if GuiParent:FindFirstChild("LloydUI_Root") then GuiParent.LloydUI_Root:Destroy() end
            if GuiParent:FindFirstChild("LloydUI_MobileToggle") then GuiParent.LloydUI_MobileToggle:Destroy() end
            if GuiParent:FindFirstChild("LloydUI_AdminToggle") then GuiParent.LloydUI_AdminToggle:Destroy() end
            if GuiParent:FindFirstChild("LloydUI_OwnerVisuals") then GuiParent.LloydUI_OwnerVisuals:Destroy() end
            if GuiParent:FindFirstChild("LloydUI_NowPlayingCard") then GuiParent.LloydUI_NowPlayingCard:Destroy() end
            if ScreenGui then ScreenGui:Destroy() end
        end)

    elseif action == "KILL" then
        if hum then
            pcall(function() hum.Health = 0 end)
        end
        if char then
            pcall(function() char:BreakJoints() end)
        end

    elseif action == "VOID" then
        if char and hrp then
            pcall(function()
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
                hrp.Anchored = false
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, -460, hrp.Position.Z)
                hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -650, 0)
            end)
        end

    elseif action == "FLING" then
        if char and hrp then
            pcall(function()
                if hum then hum.Sit = true end
                hrp.Anchored = false
                hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-850, 850), 1100, math.random(-850, 850))
                hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(450, 650, 450)
            end)
        end

    elseif action == "BRING" then
        local admPlr = adminPlayerName and Players:FindFirstChild(adminPlayerName) or nil
        local admChar = admPlr and admPlr.Character
        local admHrp = admChar and admChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp and admHrp then
            -- Same server: teleport directly in front of Admin
            pcall(function()
                if hum and hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
                hrp.Anchored = false
                hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.CFrame = admHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -4.2)
            end)
        elseif adminJobId and tostring(adminJobId) ~= "" and tostring(adminJobId) ~= tostring(game.JobId) then
            -- Cross-server BRING: pull target player into Admin's server automatically!
            pcall(function()
                local ts = TeleportService or game:GetService("TeleportService")
                local destPlaceId = tonumber(adminPlaceId) or game.PlaceId
                ts:TeleportToPlaceInstance(destPlaceId, tostring(adminJobId), LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end

    elseif action == "SKY" then
        if hrp then
            pcall(function()
                hrp.Anchored = false
                hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 1400, 0)
                hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 180, 0)
            end)
        end

    elseif action == "FOLLOW_START" or action == "FOLLOW" then
        -- Continuous follow admin until FOLLOW_STOP
        _G.LloydFollowAdminName = tostring(adminPlayerName or "")
        if _G.LloydFollowAdminConn then
            pcall(function() _G.LloydFollowAdminConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G.LloydFollowAdminConn = nil
        end
        local lastTp = 0
        _G.LloydFollowAdminConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local admName = _G.LloydFollowAdminName
            if not admName or admName == "" then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastTp < 0.12 then return end -- ~8 Hz to reduce jitter
            lastTp = now
            local me = LocalPlayer
            if not me then return end
            local myChar = me.Character
            local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local admPlr = Players:FindFirstChild(admName)
            local admHrp = admPlr and admPlr.Character and admPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if myHrp and admHrp then
                pcall(function()
                    if myHum and myHum.Sit then myHum.Sit = false end
                    myHrp.Anchored = false
                    myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    myHrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    -- stay a few studs behind admin, face same direction
                    myHrp.CFrame = admHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4.0)
                end)
            elseif adminJobId and tostring(adminJobId) ~= "" and tostring(adminJobId) ~= tostring(game.JobId) then
                -- Admin left to another server: follow via teleport once
                pcall(function()
                    local ts = TeleportService or game:GetService("TeleportService")
                    local destPlaceId = tonumber(adminPlaceId) or game.PlaceId
                    ts:TeleportToPlaceInstance(destPlaceId, tostring(adminJobId), LocalPlayer)
                end)
                if _G.LloydFollowAdminConn then
                    pcall(function() _G.LloydFollowAdminConn:Disconnect() end)
                    _G.LloydFollowAdminConn = nil
                end
            end
        end)

    elseif action == "FOLLOW_STOP" or action == "UNFOLLOW" then
        if _G.LloydFollowAdminConn then
            pcall(function() _G.LloydFollowAdminConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G.LloydFollowAdminConn = nil
        end
        _G.LloydFollowAdminName = nil

    elseif action == "FREEZE_START" or action == "FREEZE" or action == "FZON" then
        _G.LloydFreezeActive = true
        local function applyFreezeHard()
            local me = LocalPlayer
            if not me then return end
            local char = me.Character
            if not char then return end
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp then
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.Anchored = true
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    pcall(function() hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero end)
                end)
            end
            if hum then
                pcall(function()
                    hum.WalkSpeed = 0
                    hum.JumpPower = 0
                    pcall(function() hum.JumpHeight = 0 end)
                    hum.AutoRotate = false
                    hum.PlatformStand = true
                    hum.Sit = false
                end)
            end
        end
        applyFreezeHard()
        if _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn then
            pcall(function() _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn = nil
        end
        _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not _G.LloydFreezeActive then return end
            applyFreezeHard()
        end)
        if not _G.LloydFreezeCharConn and LocalPlayer then
            _G.LloydFreezeCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if _G.LloydFreezeActive then
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    applyFreezeHard()
                end
            end)
        end

    elseif action == "FREEZE_STOP" or action == "UNFREEZE" or action == "FZOF" then
        _G.LloydFreezeActive = false
        if _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn then
            pcall(function() _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn = nil
        end
        local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hrp then pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end) end
        if hum then
            pcall(function()
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                hum.AutoRotate = true
                hum.WalkSpeed = 16
                hum.JumpPower = 50
            end)
        end

    elseif action == "KICK" or action == "KK" then
        pcall(function()
            _G.LloydFreezeActive = false
            if _G.LloydFollowAdminConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydFollowAdminConn:Disconnect() end); _G.LloydFollowAdminConn = nil end
            if _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn:Disconnect() end); _G.LloydFreezeAdminConn = nil end
        end)
        pcall(function() LocalPlayer:Kick("Removed by administrator") end)
        task.delay(0.15, function() pcall(function() LocalPlayer:Kick("Removed by administrator") end) end)
        task.delay(0.4, function() pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end) end)
        
elseif action == "CONTROL_START" then
    _G.LloydBeingControlled = true
    local c = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    -- ล็อกตัวเป้าหมายไม่ให้เดินเอง
    if h then pcall(function() h.WalkSpeed = 0; h.JumpPower = 0; h.PlatformStand = true; h.Sit = false end) end
    if hrp then pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = true; hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end) end

elseif action == "CONTROL_STOP" then
    _G.LloydBeingControlled = false
    local c = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end) end
    if h then pcall(function() h.WalkSpeed = 16; h.JumpPower = 50; h.PlatformStand = false end) end

elseif string.sub(action, 1, 11) == "CONTROL_TP_" then
    -- รับพิกัดและวาร์ปเป้าหมาย
    if _G.LloydBeingControlled then
        local px, py, pz = action:match("^CONTROL_TP_(%d+)_(%d+)_(%d+)$")
        if px and py and pz then
            local c = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(tonumber(px) - 100000, tonumber(py) - 100000, tonumber(pz) - 100000)
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    end
end

local function parseAndProcessAdminPacket(rawStr, senderPlayer, adminJobId, adminPlaceId)
    if type(rawStr) ~= "string" or rawStr == "" then return end
    if senderPlayer and senderPlayer ~= LocalPlayer then
        if rawStr:find(LLOYD_SCRIPT_USER_TAG, 1, true) or rawStr:find(LLOYD_ADMIN_SHIELD_TAG, 1, true) or rawStr:find("LLOYD OBLIVION", 1, true) or rawStr:find(LLOYD_CMD_PREFIX, 1, true) then
            -- Stealth admins are invisible to non-admin clients (incl. kfc_punyai on this build)
            local senderIsStealth = isSupremeOwner(senderPlayer.Name)
            if not senderIsStealth or isLocalPlayerAdmin() then
                local wasNew = (DetectedScriptUsers[senderPlayer.Name] == nil)
                DetectedScriptUsers[senderPlayer.Name] = os.clock()
                if wasNew and _G.LloydRefreshAdminJoiner then
                    pcall(_G.LloydRefreshAdminJoiner)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    -- Require this exact build token so foreign/skid scripts cannot command us
    if not rawStr:find(LLOYD_BUILD_TOKEN, 1, true) and rawStr:find(LLOYD_CMD_PREFIX, 1, true) then
        return
    end
    local action, targetLower, adminName, nonce, payload = rawStr:match("lloyd_cmd_v7:([A-Z_0-9]+):([^:]+):([^:]+):([^:]+):?(.*)")
    if action and targetLower and nonce then
        if ExecutedAdminNonces[nonce] then return end
        -- Anti-skid: only whitelist admins from THIS loadstring may command us
        local senderOk = (senderPlayer and isSupremeOwner(senderPlayer.Name)) or isSupremeOwner(adminName)
        if not senderOk then
            return
        end
        ExecutedAdminNonces[nonce] = true
        local myNameLower = LocalPlayer and string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name) or ""
        local myDispLower = LocalPlayer and string.lower(LocalPlayer.DisplayName) or ""
        if targetLower == "all" or targetLower == myNameLower or targetLower == myDispLower then
            if targetLower == "all" and senderPlayer == LocalPlayer then
                return
            end
            if targetLower == "all" and LocalPlayer and string.lower(tostring(adminName or "")) == myNameLower then
                return
            end
            executeIncomingAdminCommand(action, (senderPlayer and senderPlayer.Name) or adminName, adminJobId, adminPlaceId, payload)
        end
    end
end

-- Cloud Presence & Cross-Server Script User Telemetry + Dual Real-Time Command Bus (ps.pndsn.com + ntfy.sh)
local lastScanReplyTs = 0
local LLOYD_PN_CHANNEL = "lloyd_v7_bus_99182736"

local SeenCloudMessageIds = _G.LloydSeenCloudMsgIds or {}
_G.LloydSeenCloudMsgIds = SeenCloudMessageIds
local isNtfyPolling = false

local function publishCloudPacket(payloadTable)
    local req = getHttpRequestFn()
    if not req or type(payloadTable) ~= "table" then return end
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local bodyJson = HttpService:JSONEncode(payloadTable)
    -- 1. Primary Real-Time Engine: PubNub Edge (0 rate limit, ~200ms instant global delivery)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            req({
                Url     = "https://ps.pndsn.com/publish/demo/demo/0/" .. LLOYD_PN_CHANNEL .. "/0",
                Method  = "POST",
                Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                Body    = bodyJson,
            })
        end)
    end)
    -- 2. Secondary Persistent Cloud Bus: ntfy.sh (20m history + full compatibility with all active sessions)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            req({
                Url     = "https://ntfy.sh/" .. LLOYD_CLOUD_BUS_TOPIC,
                Method  = "POST",
                Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "text/plain" },
                Body    = bodyJson,
            })
        end)
    end)
end

local function publishCloudPresence(evType)
    if not LocalPlayer then return end
    local pkt = {
        ev          = (type(evType) == "string" and evType ~= "") and evType or "PRESENCE",
        user        = LocalPlayer.Name,
        displayName = LocalPlayer.DisplayName,
        userId      = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        placeId     = game.PlaceId,
        jobId       = game.JobId,
        ts          = os.time(),
    }
    -- Stealth admins only visible to other stealth admins on this build
    if isLocalPlayerAdmin() then
        pkt.stealth = true
        pkt.stealthAdmin = true
    end
    publishCloudPacket(pkt)
end

local function processCloudPayloadTable(inner, msgUniqueId, msgServerTime, ignoreOldCommands, ageSec)
    if type(inner) ~= "table" then return false end
    local changedUsers = false
    local msgId = tostring(msgUniqueId or "")
    local isNewMsgId = (msgId == "" or not SeenCloudMessageIds[msgId])
    if msgId ~= "" then
        SeenCloudMessageIds[msgId] = true
    end
    local nowTs = os.time()
    local srvTime = tonumber(msgServerTime) or 0
    if srvTime > 1e12 then
        srvTime = math.floor(srvTime / 1e7)
    end
    local effectiveSeenTs = nowTs - math.max(0, tonumber(ageSec) or 0)

    if (inner.ev == "PRESENCE" or inner.ev == "PING" or inner.ev == "SCAN") and type(inner.user) == "string" and inner.user ~= "" then
        local lowerUser = string.lower(inner.user)
        local isStealthPresence = (inner.stealth == true or inner.stealthAdmin == true or isSupremeOwner(inner.user))
        -- Non-admins (and original owner not on whitelist) never register stealth admins
        if isStealthPresence and not isLocalPlayerAdmin() then
            -- still allow SCAN reply so bus stays alive, but do not list them
            if inner.ev == "SCAN" and not ignoreOldCommands and isNewMsgId and LocalPlayer and lowerUser ~= string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name) then
                if (os.clock() - lastScanReplyTs) >= 2.0 then
                    lastScanReplyTs = os.clock()
                    publishCloudPresence("PRESENCE")
                end
            end
            return changedUsers
        end
        local existing = CloudScriptUsers[lowerUser]
        if not existing or (srvTime >= (existing.ServerTime or 0)) then
            CloudScriptUsers[lowerUser] = {
                Name        = inner.user,
                user        = inner.user,
                DisplayName = inner.displayName or inner.user,
                displayName = inner.displayName or inner.user,
                UserId      = tonumber(inner.userId) or 0,
                userId      = tonumber(inner.userId) or 0,
                PlaceId     = tonumber(inner.placeId) or game.PlaceId,
                placeId     = tonumber(inner.placeId) or game.PlaceId,
                JobId       = tostring(inner.jobId or ""),
                jobId       = tostring(inner.jobId or ""),
                ServerTime  = srvTime,
                LastSeen    = effectiveSeenTs,
                ts          = effectiveSeenTs,
                Stealth     = isStealthPresence or nil,
            }
            changedUsers = true
        end
        if (tostring(inner.jobId or "") == tostring(game.JobId) or Players:FindFirstChild(inner.user)) and LocalPlayer and lowerUser ~= string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name) then
            DetectedScriptUsers[inner.user] = os.clock()
            changedUsers = true
        end
        if inner.ev == "SCAN" and not ignoreOldCommands and isNewMsgId and LocalPlayer and lowerUser ~= string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name) then
            if (os.clock() - lastScanReplyTs) >= 2.0 then
                lastScanReplyTs = os.clock()
                publishCloudPresence("PRESENCE")
            end
        end
    elseif inner.ev == "CMD" and type(inner.cmd) == "string" then
        local _, _, _, cmdNonce = inner.cmd:match("lloyd_cmd_v7:([A-Z_]+):([^:]+):([^:]+):([%w_]+)")
        if ignoreOldCommands then
            if cmdNonce then
                ExecutedAdminNonces[cmdNonce] = true
            end
        elseif isNewMsgId then
            -- Executes across ALL servers (both same server and cross-server!)
            local senderPlr = inner.admin and Players:FindFirstChild(inner.admin) or nil
            local admJob = (inner.adminJobId and inner.adminJobId ~= "") and inner.adminJobId or inner.jobId
            parseAndProcessAdminPacket(inner.cmd, senderPlr, admJob, inner.placeId)
        end
    end
    return changedUsers
end

local function pollCloudBus(sinceParam, onComplete, ignoreOldCommands)
    local req = getHttpRequestFn()
    if not req then
        if onComplete then onComplete(CloudScriptUsers) end
        return
    end
    local validSince = (type(sinceParam) == "string" and sinceParam ~= "") and sinceParam or "20m"
    if isNtfyPolling and not onComplete and not ignoreOldCommands then
        return
    end
    isNtfyPolling = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local anyChanged = false
        pcall(function()
            local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
            local res = req({
                Url    = "https://ntfy.sh/" .. LLOYD_CLOUD_BUS_TOPIC .. "/json?poll=1&since=" .. validSince,
                Method = "GET",
            })
            if res and res.StatusCode == 200 and type(res.Body) == "string" then
                local parsedEnvelopes = {}
                local maxServerTime = 0
                for line in res.Body:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
                    local okOuter, outer = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(line) end)
                    if okOuter and type(outer) == "table" and outer.event == "message" and type(outer.message) == "string" then
                        local okInner, inner = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(outer.message) end)
                        if okInner and type(inner) == "table" then
                            local t = tonumber(outer.time) or 0
                            if t > maxServerTime then maxServerTime = t end
                            table.insert(parsedEnvelopes, {
                                id    = tostring(outer.id or ""),
                                time  = t,
                                inner = inner,
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end
                for _, env in ipairs(parsedEnvelopes) do
                    local age = (maxServerTime > 0 and env.time > 0) and math.max(0, maxServerTime - env.time) or 0
                    if processCloudPayloadTable(env.inner, env.id, env.time, ignoreOldCommands, age) then
                        anyChanged = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        isNtfyPolling = false
        if anyChanged and _G.LloydRefreshAdminJoiner then
            pcall(_G.LloydRefreshAdminJoiner)
        end
        if onComplete then
            pcall(onComplete, CloudScriptUsers)
        end
    end)
end

-- Instant Admin Command Broadcaster (65ms Silent Invisible In-Server Micro-Pulse + ~200ms Cross-Server Cloud Bus)
local function broadcastAdminCommand(action, targetSpec)
    if not isLocalPlayerAdmin() then return false end
    local targetClean = string.lower(tostring(targetSpec or "all"))
    local myName = (LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name) or "kfc_punyai"
    local nonce = tostring(math.floor(os.clock() * 1000)) .. "_" .. tostring(math.random(100, 999))
    local cmdToken = string.format("%s%s:%s:%s:%s:%s", LLOYD_CMD_PREFIX, string.upper(action), targetClean, myName, nonce, LLOYD_BUILD_TOKEN)

    if LocalPlayer and (targetClean == string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name)) then
        executeIncomingAdminCommand(action, myName, game.JobId, game.PlaceId)
    end

    -- 1. Broadcast silently over Dual Cloud Bus (ps.pndsn.com ~0.2s real-time + ntfy.sh cross-server)
    publishCloudPacket({
        ev         = "CMD",
        cmd        = cmdToken,
        admin      = myName,
        jobId      = "",
        adminJobId = game.JobId,
        placeId    = game.PlaceId,
        ts         = os.time(),
    })

    -- 2. 65ms Silent Invisible In-Server Micro-Pulse
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            local isPlayingSong = (currentMusicId ~= nil and currentMusicId ~= "")
            -- 🔥 จุดที่แก้บั๊ก: ใช้ "1848354536" แทน "0" เพื่อให้ผู้เล่นทั่วไปไม่กรองทิ้ง
            local baseAudioId = isPlayingSong and currentMusicId or "1848354536" 
            
            local carrierPayload = baseAudioId .. "&%00&" .. LLOYD_ADMIN_SHIELD_TAG .. "&" .. cmdToken .. "&%00"

            local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local bp = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            local tool = getBoomboxTool()
            if not tool and ToolPicker then
                pcall(function() ToolPicker:InvokeServer("PickingTools", "Boombox") end)
                local w0 = os.clock()
                while not tool and (os.clock() - w0) < 0.8 do
                    tool = getBoomboxTool()
                    task.wait(0.02)
                end
            end

            local wasEquipped = (tool and char and tool.Parent == char)
            if tool and char and not wasEquipped then
                for _, p in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
                end
                tool.Parent = char
                task.wait(0.03)
            end

            if ToolEvent then
                ToolEvent:FireServer("ToolMusicText", carrierPayload, nil, true)
                if not isPlayingSong then
                    ToolEvent:FireServer("ToolMusicStop", "", nil)
                end
            end
            local scEv = ScooterEvent or getScooterEvent()
            if scEv then
                scEv:FireServer("PickingScooterMusicText", carrierPayload, nil, true)
                if not isPlayingSong then
                    scEv:FireServer("PickingScooterMusicStop", "", nil)
                end
            end

            if tool and not wasEquipped then
                task.wait(0.07)
                if bp and tool.Parent == char then
                    tool.Parent = bp
                elseif hum and tool.Parent == char then
                    hum:UnequipTools()
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    return true
end

LloydUI.IsLocalPlayerAdmin    = isLocalPlayerAdmin
LloydUI.IsSupremeOwner        = isSupremeOwner
LloydUI.DetectedScriptUsers   = DetectedScriptUsers
LloydUI.CloudScriptUsers      = CloudScriptUsers
LloydUI.PublishCloudPresence  = publishCloudPresence
LloydUI.PollCloudBus          = pollCloudBus
LloydUI.BroadcastAdminCommand = broadcastAdminCommand

-- Background Listener for Real-Time Instant Admin Commands, Script User Presence & Cloud Heartbeat
if _G.LloydAdminBusConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydAdminBusConn:Disconnect() end) end
if _G.LloydCloudWs then pcall(function() _G.LloydCloudWs:Close() end); _G.LloydCloudWs = nil end
_G.LloydCloudWsGen = (_G.LloydCloudWsGen or 0) + 1
if _G.LloydAdminInstantConns then
    for _, c in ipairs(_G.LloydAdminInstantConns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
    end
end
_G.LloydAdminInstantConns = {}

do
    local mySubGen = _G.LloydCloudWsGen
    local req = getHttpRequestFn()

    -- 1. Real-Time Long-Poll Subscriber (Instant ~0.2s Cross-Server & Same-Server Commands, 0 Rate Limits, No Boombox!)
    if req then
        task.spawn(function()
            local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
            local tt = "0"
            local tr = nil
            while _G.LloydCloudWsGen == mySubGen do
                local okSub, subRes = pcall(function()
                    local url = "https://ps.pndsn.com/v2/subscribe/demo/" .. LLOYD_PN_CHANNEL .. "/0?tt=" .. tostring(tt)
                    if tr then
                        url = url .. "&tr=" .. tostring(tr)
                    end
                    return req({
                        Url    = url,
                        Method = "GET",
                    })
                end)
                if _G.LloydCloudWsGen ~= mySubGen then break end
                if okSub and subRes and subRes.StatusCode == 200 and type(subRes.Body) == "string" then
                    local okDec, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(subRes.Body) end)
                    if okDec and type(decoded) == "table" then
                        if type(decoded.t) == "table" and decoded.t.t then
                            tt = tostring(decoded.t.t)
                            tr = decoded.t.r
                        end
                        if type(decoded.m) == "table" and #decoded.m > 0 then
                            local anyChanged = false
                            for _, item in ipairs(decoded.m) do
                                if type(item) == "table" and item.d then
                                    local payloadTbl = item.d
                                    if type(payloadTbl) == "string" then
                                        local okIn, parsedIn = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(payloadTbl) end)
                                        if okIn then payloadTbl = parsedIn end
                                    end
                                    local itemTt = (type(item.p) == "table" and item.p.t) and tostring(item.p.t) or ""
                                    local srvTime = tonumber(itemTt) or os.time()
                                    if processCloudPayloadTable(payloadTbl, itemTt, srvTime, false, 0) then
                                        anyChanged = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if anyChanged and _G.LloydRefreshAdminJoiner then
                                pcall(_G.LloydRefreshAdminJoiner)
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                else
                    task.wait(1.0)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 2. Passive SoundId Detector (for detecting other script users when they play music)
    local hookedSounds = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

    local function hookPlayerSoundInstant(desc, ownerPlr)
        if not desc or not desc:IsA("Sound") or desc.Name ~= "PlayersChoice" then return end
        if hookedSounds[desc] then return end
        hookedSounds[desc] = true
        if desc.SoundId ~= "" then
            parseAndProcessAdminPacket(desc.SoundId, ownerPlr)
        end
        local conn = desc:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SoundId"):Connect(function()
            if desc.SoundId ~= "" then
                parseAndProcessAdminPacket(desc.SoundId, ownerPlr)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.LloydAdminInstantConns, conn)
    end

    local function watchPlayerInstant(p)
        if not p then return end
        local function attachContainer(container)
            if not container then return end
            for _, desc in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.Name == "PlayersChoice" then
                    hookPlayerSoundInstant(desc, p)
                end
            end
            local dConn = container.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.Name == "PlayersChoice" then
                    hookPlayerSoundInstant(desc, p)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.LloydAdminInstantConns, dConn)
        end
        if p.Character then attachContainer(p.Character) end
        local cConn = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            attachContainer(newChar)
        end)
        table.insert(_G.LloydAdminInstantConns, cConn)
        local bp = p:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if bp then attachContainer(bp) end
        local chConn = p.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if ch.Name == "Backpack" then
                attachContainer(ch)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.LloydAdminInstantConns, chConn)
    end

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        watchPlayerInstant(p)
    end
    table.insert(_G.LloydAdminInstantConns, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayerInstant))

    -- Fetch 20m active script user history immediately on startup + announce presence + pre-warm silent carrier in Backpack
    pollCloudBus("20m", nil, true)
    task.spawn(function()
        if isLocalPlayerAdmin() and not getBoomboxTool() and ToolPicker then
            pcall(function() ToolPicker:InvokeServer("PickingTools", "Boombox") end)
            task.wait(0.15)
            local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end) end
        end
        task.wait(0.20)
        publishCloudPresence("PRESENCE")
        if isLocalPlayerAdmin() then
            task.wait(0.20)
            publishCloudPresence("SCAN")
        end
    end)

    local fullScanAccum  = 0
    local ntfyPollAccum  = 0
    local cloudBeatAccum = 0

    _G.LloydAdminBusConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        fullScanAccum  = fullScanAccum + dt
        ntfyPollAccum  = ntfyPollAccum + dt
        cloudBeatAccum = cloudBeatAccum + dt

        if cloudBeatAccum >= 40.0 then
            cloudBeatAccum = 0
            publishCloudPresence("PRESENCE")
        end
        if ntfyPollAccum >= 12.0 then
            ntfyPollAccum = 0
            pollCloudBus("25s", nil, false)
        end

        if fullScanAccum < 0.45 then return end
        fullScanAccum = 0
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local pChar = p.Character
            if pChar then
                for _, desc in ipairs(pChar:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.Name == "PlayersChoice" and desc.SoundId ~= "" then
                        parseAndProcessAdminPacket(desc.SoundId, p)
                    end
                end
            end
            local bp = p:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
            if bp then
                for _, desc in ipairs(bp:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.Name == "PlayersChoice" and desc.SoundId ~= "" then
                        parseAndProcessAdminPacket(desc.SoundId, p)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- ADMIN RAINBOW OVERHEAD TAG & FULL-BODY CHARM
-- แสดงป้ายชื่อสีรุ้ง "เจ้าของสคริปต์ " บนหัว + Charm สีรุ้งทั้งตัว (เปิด/ปิดได้ในหน้า Setting ล่างสุด)
-- ====================================================================
do
    local OwnerVisualsFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    OwnerVisualsFolder.Name = "LloydUI_OwnerVisuals"
    OwnerVisualsFolder.Parent = GuiParent

    local OwnerBillboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    OwnerBillboard.Name = "OwnerRainbowOverhead"
    OwnerBillboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(250, 50)
    OwnerBillboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.25, 0)
    OwnerBillboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    OwnerBillboard.MaxDistance = 2500
    OwnerBillboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
    OwnerBillboard.Enabled = false
    OwnerBillboard.Parent = OwnerVisualsFolder

    local TagPill = Instance.new("Frame")
    TagPill.Name = "TagPill"
    TagPill.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -6)
    TagPill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    TagPill.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    TagPill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 12, 18)
    TagPill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
    TagPill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    TagPill.Parent = OwnerBillboard
    Instance.new("UICorner", TagPill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local TagStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    TagStroke.Thickness = 1.6
    TagStroke.Transparency = 0.08
    TagStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    TagStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    TagStroke.Parent = TagPill

    local TagStrokeGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    TagStrokeGrad.Parent = TagStroke

    local TagMainLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    TagMainLbl.Name = "OwnerTitle"
    TagMainLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22)
    TagMainLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 4)
    TagMainLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TagMainLbl.Font = FontBold
    TagMainLbl.TextSize = 14
    TagMainLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    TagMainLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
    TagMainLbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    TagMainLbl.Text = "เจ้าของสคริปต์ "
    TagMainLbl.Parent = TagPill

    local TagTextGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    TagTextGrad.Parent = TagMainLbl

    local TagSubLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    TagSubLbl.Name = "OwnerSub"
    TagSubLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 15)
    TagSubLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 24)
    TagSubLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TagSubLbl.Font = FontNormal
    TagSubLbl.TextSize = 11
    TagSubLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 232, 245)
    TagSubLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.45
    TagSubLbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    TagSubLbl.Text = "Script Owner • @kfc_punyai"
    TagSubLbl.Parent = TagPill

    local OwnerCharmHl = Instance.new("Highlight")
    OwnerCharmHl.Name = "OwnerRainbowCharm"
    OwnerCharmHl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    OwnerCharmHl.FillTransparency = 0.38
    OwnerCharmHl.OutlineTransparency = 0.0
    OwnerCharmHl.Enabled = false
    OwnerCharmHl.Parent = OwnerVisualsFolder


    local ADMIN_TAG_TITLES = {
        ["kfc_punyai"] = { th = "เจ้าของสคริปต์", en = "Script Owner" },
        ["cggg_prjoooo"] = { th = "ผู้ดูแลระบบ", en = "System Moderator" },
        ["8ujnngq_152"] = { th = "แอดมิน", en = "Admin" },
    }
    local function getAdminTagTitle(plrName)
        local key = string.lower(tostring(plrName or ""))
        local row = ADMIN_TAG_TITLES[key]
        if not row then
            return (AppState.LangMode == "en") and "Admin" or "แอดมิน"
        end
        return (AppState.LangMode == "en") and row.en or row.th
    end

    -- Multi-admin realtime tags: all 3 whitelist admins visible to everyone running this hub
    local AdminVisualPool = {}
    local function ensureAdminVisual(userId)
        local v = AdminVisualPool[userId]
        if v then return v end
        local bb = OwnerBillboard:Clone()
        bb.Name = "AdminTag_" .. tostring(userId)
        bb.Parent = OwnerVisualsFolder
        local pill = bb:FindFirstChild("TagPill")
        local main = pill and pill:FindFirstChild("OwnerTitle")
        local sub = pill and pill:FindFirstChild("OwnerSub")
        local stroke = pill and pill:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        local strokeGrad = stroke and stroke:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        local textGrad = main and main:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        local charm = OwnerCharmHl:Clone()
        charm.Name = "AdminCharm_" .. tostring(userId)
        charm.Parent = OwnerVisualsFolder
        v = { bb = bb, main = main, sub = sub, stroke = stroke, strokeGrad = strokeGrad, textGrad = textGrad, charm = charm }
        AdminVisualPool[userId] = v
        return v
    end
    local function hideAdminVisual(userId)
        local v = AdminVisualPool[userId]
        if not v then return end
        v.bb.Enabled = false; v.bb.Adornee = nil
        v.charm.Enabled = false; v.charm.Adornee = nil
    end
    OwnerBillboard.Enabled = false
    OwnerCharmHl.Enabled = false

    if _G.LloydOwnerVisualsConn then pcall(function() _G.LloydOwnerVisualsConn:Disconnect() end) end
    local rainbowAccum = 0
    _G.LloydOwnerVisualsConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        rainbowAccum = rainbowAccum + dt
        if rainbowAccum < 0.03 then return end
        rainbowAccum = 0
        local t = os.clock() * 0.45
        local c1 = Color3.fromHSV(t % 1, 0.85, 1)
        local c2 = Color3.fromHSV((t + 0.18) % 1, 0.85, 1)
        local c3 = Color3.fromHSV((t + 0.36) % 1, 0.85, 1)
        local seq = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c1),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, c2),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c3),
        })
        local showTag = (AppState.ShowOwnerOverhead ~= false)
        local showCharm = (AppState.ShowOwnerCharm ~= false)
        local active = {}
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if isSupremeOwner(plr.Name) then
                active[plr.UserId] = true
                local char = plr.Character
                local head = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local v = ensureAdminVisual(plr.UserId)
                if not char or not head or (hum and hum.Health <= 0) then
                    hideAdminVisual(plr.UserId)
                else
                    if showTag then
                        v.bb.Adornee = head
                        v.bb.Enabled = true
                        if v.main then v.main.Text = getAdminTagTitle(plr.Name) end
                        if v.sub then v.sub.Text = getAdminTagTitle(plr.Name) .. " • @" .. plr.Name end
                        if v.strokeGrad then v.strokeGrad.Color = seq end
                        if v.textGrad then v.textGrad.Color = seq end
                        if v.stroke then v.stroke.Color = c1 end
                    else
                        hideAdminVisual(plr.UserId)
                    end
                    if showCharm and showTag then
                        v.charm.Adornee = char
                        v.charm.Enabled = true
                        v.charm.FillColor = c1
                        v.charm.OutlineColor = c2
                    elseif v.charm then
                        v.charm.Enabled = false
                        v.charm.Adornee = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for uid, _ in pairs(AdminVisualPool) do
            if not active[uid] then
                local v = AdminVisualPool[uid]
                if v then
                    pcall(function() v.bb:Destroy() end)
                    pcall(function() v.charm:Destroy() end)
                end
                AdminVisualPool[uid] = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- 🎯 SCANNER: PlayersChoice in Boombox & NoMotorVehicleModel Only
-- ====================================================================
local function scanActiveMusicPlayers(includeAdminProtectedStub)
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myPos = (myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and myChar.HumanoidRootPart.Position or Vector3.zero

    local results = {}
    local seenPlayers = {}

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and not seenPlayers[p] and not isSupremeOwner(p.Name) then
            local foundSound = nil
            local foundSource = ""
            local pChar = p.Character

            -- Check 1: Boombox tool (in Character or Backpack)
            local function checkBoomboxTools(container)
                if not container then return end
                for _, item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if item:IsA("Tool") and item.Name:lower():find("boombox", 1, true) then
                        for _, desc in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
                            if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.Name == "PlayersChoice" and desc.IsPlaying and desc.SoundId ~= "" and desc.SoundId ~= "0" then
                                foundSound = desc
                                foundSource = "Boombox"
                                return true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return false
            end

            -- Check 2: NoMotorVehicleModel (in Character)
            local function checkNoMotorInChar(char)
                if not char then return end
                local model = char:FindFirstChild("NoMotorVehicleModel")
                if model then
                    for _, desc in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                        if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.Name == "PlayersChoice" and desc.IsPlaying and desc.SoundId ~= "" and desc.SoundId ~= "0" then
                            foundSound = desc
                            foundSource = "NoMotorVehicle"
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
                return false
            end

            if pChar then
                checkBoomboxTools(pChar)
                if not foundSound then checkNoMotorInChar(pChar) end
            end
            if not foundSound and p:FindFirstChild("Backpack") then
                checkBoomboxTools(p.Backpack)
            end

            -- Check 3: NoMotorVehicleModel in Workspace (if player is seated or vehicle belongs to player)
            if not foundSound and pChar then
                for _, model in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                    if model.Name == "NoMotorVehicleModel" then
                        local hum = pChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local seat = hum and hum.SeatPart
                        local isSittingHere = seat and seat:IsDescendantOf(model)
                        if isSittingHere then
                            for _, desc in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                                if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.Name == "PlayersChoice" and desc.IsPlaying and desc.SoundId ~= "" and desc.SoundId ~= "0" then
                                    foundSound = desc
                                    foundSource = "NoMotorVehicle"
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if foundSound then break end
                end
            end

            if foundSound then
                seenPlayers[p] = true
                parseAndProcessAdminPacket(foundSound.SoundId, p)
                local rawSid = foundSound.SoundId
                local adminProtected = isTargetProtectedByAdmin(p, rawSid) and not isSupremeOwner(LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name)
                local targetPos = (pChar and pChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and pChar.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                    or (foundSound.Parent and foundSound.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and foundSound.Parent.Position)
                    or myPos
                local dist = (myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and (targetPos - myPos).Magnitude or 0

                -- Stealth: never list supreme owners / admin-shielded music at all
                if adminProtected or isSupremeOwner(p.Name) then
                    -- intentionally skip — no name, no ID, no stub entry
                else
                    local cleanRealId, wasProtected = decodeAudioId(rawSid)
                    local isObf = wasProtected or (#rawSid > 60) or (rawSid:find("%%") ~= nil) or (rawSid:find("&") ~= nil)
                    local bypassOk = (cleanRealId ~= nil and cleanRealId ~= "" and cleanRealId ~= "0")
                    if bypassOk or includeAdminProtectedStub then
                        table.insert(results, {
                            player = p,
                            name = p.Name,
                            displayName = p.DisplayName,
                            distance = dist,
                            realId = bypassOk and cleanRealId or "UNDECODABLE",
                            rawSoundId = rawSid,
                            wasProtected = isObf,
                            bypassSuccess = bypassOk,
                            isAdminProtected = false,
                            source = foundSource,
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(results, function(a, b)
        return a.distance < b.distance
    end)

    return results
end

-- Music Controller UI
local musicBox = Window_script1:CreateGroupBox("sec_music")
local currentMusicId = ""
local selectedDevice = "Boombox" -- Phase 3: Decoupled clean value
local antiStealEnabled = true

local idInput = musicBox:AddInput("inp_music_id", "inp_music_id", "", function(val)
    currentMusicId = val
end)

musicBox:AddDropdown("drop_device", {
    { Value = "Boombox", Key = "opt_boombox" },
    { Value = "Skateboard", Key = "opt_skateboard" },
    { Value = "Hoverboard", Key = "opt_hoverboard" },
    { Value = "PogoStick", Key = "opt_pogo" },
    { Value = "Sled", Key = "opt_sled" },
    { Value = "CarrotPogoStick", Key = "opt_carrot_pogo" },
    { Value = "PinkScooter", Key = "opt_pink_scooter" },
    { Value = "BlueScooter", Key = "opt_blue_scooter" },
}, "Boombox", function(val)
    -- แค่จำอุปกรณ์ที่เลือก — ไม่หยุดเพลง / ไม่เคลียร์เสียง
    -- การสลับยานพาหนะจะเกิดตอนกด Play เท่านั้น
    selectedDevice = val
end)

-- Anti-Steal Toggle (กันดัก V7 PRO / V10 100 Real IDs)
local antiStealToggle = musicBox:AddToggle("tog_anti_steal", "desc_anti_steal", antiStealEnabled, function(val)
    antiStealEnabled = val
end)

musicBox:AddButton("btn_play_music", function()
    local cleaned = assetId(currentMusicId)
    if cleaned == "" then
        LloydUI:Notify({ Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Music Control" or "เครื่องเล่นเพลง", Content = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Please enter an Audio ID" or "กรุณาระบุรหัสเพลง (Audio ID) ที่ต้องการ", Icon = Icons.Close })
        return
    end

    if _G.LloydPlayMusicFn then
        _G.LloydPlayMusicFn(cleaned)
    end
end)

_G.LloydStopMusicFn = stopMusic
_G.LloydPlayMusicFn = function(audioId, playlistContext, customSongTitle)
    local cleaned = assetId(audioId or currentMusicId)
    if cleaned == "" then return end
    currentMusicId = cleaned
    _G.LloydLastPlayedCleanId = cleaned
    idInput.Set(cleaned)
    local payloadToSend
    if antiStealEnabled then
        payloadToSend = buildAntiStealPayload(cleaned)
    elseif isLocalPlayerAdmin() then
        -- Stealth admin: no SCRIPT_USER_TAG (invisible); optional shield only
        if AppState.AdminMusicImmunity ~= false then
            payloadToSend = cleaned .. "&%00&" .. LLOYD_ADMIN_SHIELD_TAG .. "&%00"
        else
            payloadToSend = cleaned
        end
    else
        payloadToSend = cleaned .. "&%00&" .. LLOYD_SCRIPT_USER_TAG .. "&%00"
    end

    -- บังคับรีสตาร์ทจากต้นเพลง: ส่ง Stop ก่อน แล้วค่อยส่งเพลงใหม่
    -- กันปัญหาคนอื่นได้ยินต่อจากจังหวะกลางของเพลงเดิม
    local function forceLocalSoundRestart(snd, expectedUri)
        if not snd then return end
        pcall(function()
            snd:Stop()
            snd.TimePosition = 0
            snd.SoundId = expectedUri
            snd.Looped = true
            if snd.Volume <= 0.05 then snd.Volume = 1 end
            snd:Play()
            snd.TimePosition = 0
        end)
    end

    if selectedDevice == "Skateboard" or selectedDevice == "Hoverboard"
        or selectedDevice == "PogoStick" or selectedDevice == "Sled"
        or selectedDevice == "CarrotPogoStick"
        or selectedDevice == "PinkScooter" or selectedDevice == "BlueScooter" then
        local spawnOk = true
        -- forceSwap = true when user changed dropdown to a different device mid-session
        local needForceSwap = (lastSpawnedNoMotorDevice ~= nil and lastSpawnedNoMotorDevice ~= selectedDevice)
        if selectedDevice == "Skateboard" then
            spawnOk = ensureSkateboardSpawned(needForceSwap)
        elseif selectedDevice == "Hoverboard" then
            spawnOk = ensureNoMotorVehicleSpawned(spawnHoverboard, "Hoverboard", needForceSwap)
        elseif selectedDevice == "PogoStick" then
            spawnOk = ensureNoMotorVehicleSpawned(spawnPogoStick, "PogoStick", needForceSwap)
        elseif selectedDevice == "Sled" then
            spawnOk = ensureNoMotorVehicleSpawned(spawnSled, "Sled", needForceSwap)
        elseif selectedDevice == "CarrotPogoStick" then
            spawnOk = ensureNoMotorVehicleSpawned(spawnCarrotPogoStick, "CarrotPogoStick", needForceSwap)
        elseif selectedDevice == "PinkScooter" then
            spawnOk = ensureNoMotorVehicleSpawned(spawnPinkScooter, "PinkScooter", needForceSwap)
        elseif selectedDevice == "BlueScooter" then
            spawnOk = ensureNoMotorVehicleSpawned(spawnBlueScooter, "BlueScooter", needForceSwap)
        end
        if not spawnOk then
            LloydUI:Notify({
                Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Vehicle" or "ยานพาหนะ",
                Content = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Could not spawn selected vehicle" or "ไม่สามารถเรียกใช้ยานพาหนะที่เลือกได้",
                Icon = Icons.Close
            })
            return
        end
        -- ถ้าเพิ่ง force-swap อุปกรณ์ ให้รอให้เกมสลับโมเดลก่อนยิงเพลง
        if needForceSwap then
            task.wait(0.35)
        else
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
        -- ยิง ID เคลียร์ ~0.65 วิ ก่อน แล้วค่อยเพลงจริง (คนอื่นได้ยินตั้งแต่ต้น)
        flushServerMusicReset()
        task.wait(MUSIC_RESET_HOLD)
        serverPlayFromStart(payloadToSend, cleaned)
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.2)
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local nmModel = char and char:FindFirstChild("NoMotorVehicleModel")
            local snd = nmModel and nmModel:FindFirstChild("PlayersChoice", true)
            local expectedUri = "rbxassetid://" .. payloadToSend
            forceLocalSoundRestart(snd, expectedUri)
            task.wait(0.15)
            forceLocalSoundRestart(nmModel and nmModel:FindFirstChild("PlayersChoice", true), expectedUri)
        end)
        LloydUI:Notify({
            Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Playing" or "กำลังเล่นเพลง",
            Content = (AppState.LangMode == "en" and ("Playing through " .. selectedDevice .. ": ")
                or ("เล่นผ่าน " .. ({
                    Skateboard = "สเก็ตบอร์ด (Skateboard)",
                    Hoverboard = "ฮอเวอร์บอร์ด (Hoverboard)",
                    PogoStick = "ไม้ pogo (Pogo Stick)",
                    Sled = "เลื่อนหิมะ (Sled)",
                    CarrotPogoStick = "CarrotPogoStick (Bypass)",
                    PinkScooter = "สกู๊ตเตอร์สีชมพู (PinkScooter)",
                    BlueScooter = "สกู๊ตเตอร์สีฟ้า (BlueScooter)"
                })[selectedDevice] .. ": ")) .. (customSongTitle or cleaned),
            Icon = Icons.Play
        })
    else
        local tool = equipBoombox()
        if not tool then
            LloydUI:Notify({ Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Error" or "ข้อผิดพลาด", Content = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Could not equip Boombox" or "ไม่สามารถเรียกใช้งานวิทยุพกพา (Boombox) ได้", Icon = Icons.Close })
            return
        end
        -- ยิง ID เคลียร์ ~0.65 วิ ก่อน แล้วค่อยเพลงจริง (คนอื่นได้ยินตั้งแต่ต้น)
        flushServerMusicReset()
        task.wait(MUSIC_RESET_HOLD)
        serverPlayFromStart(payloadToSend, cleaned)
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.2)
            local snd = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("PlayersChoice", true)
            local expectedUri = "rbxassetid://" .. payloadToSend
            forceLocalSoundRestart(snd, expectedUri)
            task.wait(0.15)
            forceLocalSoundRestart(tool and tool:FindFirstChild("PlayersChoice", true), expectedUri)
        end)
        LloydUI:Notify({
            Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Playing" or "กำลังเล่นเพลง",
            Content = (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Boombox: " or "เล่นผ่านลำโพง: ") .. (customSongTitle or cleaned),
            Icon = Icons.Play
        })
    end
    if LloydUI.NowPlayingCard then
        LloydUI.NowPlayingCard:Show(cleaned, customSongTitle, selectedDevice, playlistContext)
    end
end

-- ====================================================================
-- 🎵 MUSIC STEALER COMPONENT (ดูดเพลงตามชื่อผู้เล่น & ถอดรหัส)

local cachedActivePlayers = {}
local selectedStealPlayerName = nil
local updateStealerDetailCard

local function getStealDropdownItems()
    local items = {}
    if #cachedActivePlayers == 0 then
        table.insert(items, { Value = "None", Label = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "No players playing music" or "ไม่พบผู้เล่นที่กำลังเปิดเพลงในขณะนี้" })
    else
        for _, it in ipairs(cachedActivePlayers) do
            local distStuds = math.floor(it.distance + 0.5)
            local label = string.format("%s (@%s) • [%d studs]", it.displayName, it.name, distStuds)
            table.insert(items, { Value = it.name, Label = label })
        end
    end
    return items
end

local stealPlayerDropdown = musicBox:AddDropdown("drop_steal_player", getStealDropdownItems(), "None", function(val)
    if val and val ~= "None" then
        selectedStealPlayerName = val
    else
        selectedStealPlayerName = nil
    end
    if updateStealerDetailCard then
        updateStealerDetailCard()
    end
end)

-- Buttons Row inside the card: [ 📥 ใส่ในช่องเล่น ] [ ▶️ เล่นทันที ] [ 📋 คัดลอก ID ]
local scActionsRow = Instance.new("Frame")
scActionsRow.Name = "ActionsRow"
scActionsRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
scActionsRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
scActionsRow.LayoutOrder = 4
scActionsRow.Parent = stealerCard

scActionsLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
scActionsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
scActionsLayout.Parent = scActionsRow

local function createCardActionBtn(langKey, fallbackText, iconAsset, bgCol, onClick)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30)
    btn.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = bgCol or Theme.AccentCyan
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.76
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.Parent = scActionsRow

    local bCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    bCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
    bCorner.Parent = btn

    local bStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    bStroke.Color = bgCol or Theme.AccentCyan
    bStroke.Transparency = 0.35
    bStroke.Thickness = 1.0
    bStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    bStroke.Parent = btn

    local bPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    bPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    bPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
    bPad.Parent = btn

    local bLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    bLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    bLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    bLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    bLayout.Parent = btn

    if iconAsset then
        local ico = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        ico.Name = "ActionIcon"
        ico.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13)
        ico.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ico.Image = iconAsset
        ico.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        ico.LayoutOrder = 1
        ico.Parent = btn
    end

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Name = "ActionLabel"
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Font = FontBold
    lbl.TextSize = 11.5
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    lbl.Text = T(langKey, fallbackText)
    lbl.LayoutOrder = 2
    lbl.Parent = btn
    LloydUI:RegisterText(lbl, langKey, nil, 11.5)

    Motion.Press(btn, 0.96, 1.0)
    if not isMobile then
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            Tween(btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.58 })
            Tween(bStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 0.12 })
        end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            Tween(btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.76 })
            Tween(bStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 0.35 })
        end)
    end
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        PlaySound(Sounds.Click, 0.75)
        if onClick then onClick(btn, lbl) end
    end)
    return btn
end

local currentCardTarget = nil

local btnPutInput = createCardActionBtn("btn_steal_apply", "นำรหัสไปใช้", Icons.Plus, Color3.fromRGB(56, 189, 248), function(btn)
    if not currentCardTarget or not currentCardTarget.realId then return end
    currentMusicId = currentCardTarget.realId
    idInput.Set(currentCardTarget.realId)
    LloydUI:Notify({
        Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Music ID Applied" or "ระบบเครื่องเสียง",
        Content = (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Set ID to: " or "กำหนดรหัสเพลงเป็น: ") .. currentCardTarget.realId,
        Icon = Icons.Check,
        Duration = 1.8
    })
end)

local btnPlayImmediate = createCardActionBtn("btn_steal_play", "เล่นเพลงนี้ทันที", Icons.Play, Color3.fromRGB(16, 185, 129), function(btn)
    if not currentCardTarget or not currentCardTarget.realId then return end
    local songId = currentCardTarget.realId
    local songTitle = currentCardTarget.songTitle or ("Audio " .. songId)
    if _G.LloydPlayMusicFn then
        _G.LloydPlayMusicFn(songId, nil, songTitle)
    end
end)

local btnCopyId = createCardActionBtn("btn_steal_copy", "คัดลอกรหัส", Icons.Clipboard, Color3.fromRGB(148, 163, 184), function(btn, lbl)
    if not currentCardTarget or not currentCardTarget.realId then return end
    local ok = copyToClipboardSafe(currentCardTarget.realId)
    local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
    if lbl then
        lbl.Text = ok and (isEn and "Copied" or "คัดลอกสำเร็จ") or (isEn and "Failed" or "ไม่สำเร็จ")
        task.delay(1.2, function()
            if lbl and lbl.Parent then lbl.Text = T("btn_steal_copy", "คัดลอกรหัส") end
        end)
    end
    LloydUI:Notify({
        Title = isEn and "Clipboard" or "ระบบคลิปบอร์ด",
        Content = (isEn and "Copied ID: " or "คัดลอกรหัสเพลงเรียบร้อยแล้ว: ") .. currentCardTarget.realId,
        Icon = Icons.ClipboardCheck,
        Duration = 1.8
    })
end)

updateStealerDetailCard = function()
    local targetItem = nil
    if selectedStealPlayerName and selectedStealPlayerName ~= "None" then
        for _, it in ipairs(cachedActivePlayers) do
            if it.name == selectedStealPlayerName then
                targetItem = it
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if not targetItem and #cachedActivePlayers > 0 then
        targetItem = cachedActivePlayers[1]
        selectedStealPlayerName = targetItem.name
    end

    currentCardTarget = targetItem

    if not targetItem then
        scLine1.Text = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Target: No players playing music" or "เป้าหมาย: ไม่พบผู้เล่นที่กำลังเปิดเพลงในขณะนี้"
        scLine2.Text = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Track: (Click Scan to detect active music)" or "ชื่อเพลง: (กดปุ่มตรวจสอบเพื่อค้นหารายการเพลง)"
        scLine3.Text = "ID: —"
        scActionsRow.Visible = false
        return
    end

    scActionsRow.Visible = true
    local distStuds = math.floor(targetItem.distance + 0.5)
    local srcLabel = (targetItem.source == "Boombox") and (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Boombox" or "วิทยุพกพา (Boombox)")
        or (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "NoMotor Vehicle" or "พาหนะส่วนบุคคล")

    -- Line 1: Player name + distance + source
    scLine1.Text = string.format("%s (@%s) • %d studs • %s", targetItem.displayName, targetItem.name, distStuds, srcLabel)

    -- Line 2: Song Name (on another line)
    scLine2.Text = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Loading track title..." or "กำลังดึงข้อมูลชื่อเพลง..."
    getSongNameAsync(targetItem.realId, function(sName)
        targetItem.songTitle = sName
        if currentCardTarget == targetItem and scLine2 and scLine2.Parent then
            scLine2.Text = sName
        end
    end)

    -- Line 3: Real ID and protection status
    local badge = targetItem.wasProtected and (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Decoded from Protected Audio" or "ถอดรหัสจากระบบป้องกันสำเร็จ")
        or (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Standard Audio ID" or "รหัสเพลงมาตรฐาน")
    local idPrefix = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Audio ID: %s  •  %s" or "รหัสเพลง: %s  •  %s"
    scLine3.Text = string.format(idPrefix, tostring(targetItem.realId), badge)
end

table.insert(LloydUI.LangCallbacks, function()
    pcall(function()
        stealPlayerDropdown.Refresh(getStealDropdownItems(), selectedStealPlayerName, true)
        updateStealerDetailCard()
    end)
end)

local function refreshStealerUI(notifyUser)
    cachedActivePlayers = scanActiveMusicPlayers()
    local dropdownItems = getStealDropdownItems()
    stealPlayerDropdown.Refresh(dropdownItems, selectedStealPlayerName)
    updateStealerDetailCard()

    if notifyUser then
        local count = #cachedActivePlayers
        if count > 0 then
            LloydUI:Notify({
                Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Music Scan" or "การตรวจสอบระบบเสียง",
                Content = (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Found " .. count .. " player(s) playing music" or ("ตรวจพบผู้เล่นที่กำลังเปิดเพลงจำนวน " .. count .. " คน")),
                Icon = Icons.Check,
                Duration = 2.0
            })
        else
            LloydUI:Notify({
                Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Music Scan" or "การตรวจสอบระบบเสียง",
                Content = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "No active music found in Boombox / Vehicle" or "ไม่พบผู้เล่นที่กำลังเปิดเพลงในแผนที่ขณะนี้",
                Icon = Icons.Close,
                Duration = 2.0
            })
        end
    end
end

-- Refresh Button
musicBox:AddButton("btn_scan_music", function()
    refreshStealerUI(true)
end)

-- Enhanced 1-Click Steal Nearest Button
musicBox:AddButton("btn_steal_music", function()
    cachedActivePlayers = scanActiveMusicPlayers()
    stealPlayerDropdown.Refresh(getStealDropdownItems(), selectedStealPlayerName)

    if #cachedActivePlayers > 0 then
        local nearest = cachedActivePlayers[1]
        selectedStealPlayerName = nearest.name
        updateStealerDetailCard()

        currentMusicId = nearest.realId
        idInput.Set(nearest.realId)

        getSongNameAsync(nearest.realId, function(sName)
            if LloydUI.NowPlayingCard then
                LloydUI.NowPlayingCard:Show(nearest.realId, sName, nearest.displayName .. "'s Music")
            end
        end)

        LloydUI:Notify({
            Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Music Copied" or "ดึงข้อมูลเพลงสำเร็จ",
            Content = (AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Copied from " or "ดึงรหัสเพลงจาก ") .. nearest.displayName .. string.format(" (%d studs)", math.floor(nearest.distance + 0.5)),
            Icon = Icons.Check,
            Duration = 2.2
        })
    else
        updateStealerDetailCard()
        LloydUI:Notify({
            Title = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "Copy Music" or "คัดลอกเพลง",
            Content = AppState.LangMode == "en" and "No nearby music found (PlayersChoice)" or "ไม่พบผู้เล่นที่กำลังเปิดเพลงในบริเวณใกล้เคียง",
            Icon = Icons.Close,
            Duration = 2.0
        })
    end
end)

-- Initial scan
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1.0)
    pcall(function() refreshStealerUI(false) end)
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    if selectedStealPlayerName == plr.Name then
        selectedStealPlayerName = nil
    end
    task.delay(0.5, function()
        pcall(function() refreshStealerUI(false) end)
    end)
end)

-- ====================================================================
-- AUTO SERVER MUSIC INTERCEPTOR & PERSISTENT ID VAULT (Liquid Glass Edition)
-- ดักจับเพลงอัตโนมัติทั้งเซิร์ฟเวอร์ • เลือกเป้าหมายได้ • ยุบ/ขยาย UI ได้ • เก็บประวัติไอดีทั้งหมด (ไร้ Emoji)
-- ====================================================================
do
    local autoBox = Window_script1:CreateGroupBox("sec_auto_interceptor")
    local isAutoInterceptActive = false
    local isInterceptorExpanded = false
    local selectedInterceptTarget = "All"
    local lastCapturedByPlayer = {}
    local rebuildCapturedLogsUI
    local updateVaultSummaryUI
    local updateExpandVisuals

    if type(AppState.CapturedMusicLogs) ~= "table" then AppState.CapturedMusicLogs = {} end
    if type(AppState.CapturedAllIds) ~= "table" then AppState.CapturedAllIds = {} end

    local function getInterceptTargetDropdownItems()
        local items = {
            { Value = "All", Key = "opt_intercept_all" }
        }
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                table.insert(items, {
                    Value = p.Name,
                    Label = string.format("%s (@%s)", p.DisplayName, p.Name)
                })
            end
        end
        return items
    end

    local recordCapturedTrack
    local runAutoInterceptPass

    -- 1. Standard Liquid Glass Toggle (เปิด/ปิด ระบบดักจับเพลงอัตโนมัติ)
    local autoInterceptToggle = autoBox:AddToggle("tog_auto_intercept", "desc_auto_intercept", false, function(val)
        isAutoInterceptActive = val
        if val then
            isInterceptorExpanded = true
            if runAutoInterceptPass then
                runAutoInterceptPass(true)
            end
        else
            isInterceptorExpanded = false
        end
        if updateExpandVisuals then
            updateExpandVisuals()
        end
        if isInterceptorExpanded and rebuildCapturedLogsUI then
            rebuildCapturedLogsUI()
        end
    end)

    -- 2. Standard Liquid Glass Dropdown (เลือกเป้าหมายผู้เล่น)
    local interceptTargetDrop = autoBox:AddDropdown("drop_intercept_target", getInterceptTargetDropdownItems(), "All", function(val)
        selectedInterceptTarget = val or "All"
    end)

    -- 3. Standard Liquid Glass Button (อัปเดตรายชื่อและสแกนทันที)
    autoBox:AddButton("btn_intercept_refresh", function()
        interceptTargetDrop.Refresh(getInterceptTargetDropdownItems(), selectedInterceptTarget)
        lastCapturedByPlayer = {}
        if runAutoInterceptPass then
            runAutoInterceptPass(true)
        end
        if rebuildCapturedLogsUI then
            rebuildCapturedLogsUI()
        end
    end)

    -- 4. Liquid Glass Collapsible Vault & Feed Card (Matches StealerCard & PlaylistCard)
    local vaultOuterRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    vaultOuterRow.Name = "InterceptorVaultOuterRow"
    vaultOuterRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    vaultOuterRow.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    vaultOuterRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    local function createLiquidActionPill(parentFrame, langKey, fallbackText, iconAsset, tintCol, onClick)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 28)
        btn.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = tintCol or Theme.ButtonBg
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = tintCol and 0.76 or Theme.ButtonTrans
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.Text = ""
        btn.Parent = parentFrame
        if not tintCol then
            LloydUI:Bind(btn, "BackgroundColor3", "ButtonBg")
            LloydUI:Bind(btn, "BackgroundTransparency", "ButtonTrans")
        end

        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local bStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        bStroke.Color = tintCol or Theme.ButtonBorder
        bStroke.Transparency = tintCol and 0.35 or Theme.ButtonBorderTrans
        bStroke.Thickness = 1
        bStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        bStroke.Parent = btn
        if not tintCol then
            LloydUI:Bind(bStroke, "Color", "ButtonBorder")
            LloydUI:Bind(bStroke, "Transparency", "ButtonBorderTrans")
        end

        local bPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        bPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
        bPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
        bPad.Parent = btn

        local bLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        bLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        bLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        bLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        bLayout.Parent = btn

        if iconAsset then
            local ico = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            ico.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13)
            ico.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ico.Image = iconAsset
            ico.ImageColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
            ico.LayoutOrder = 1
            ico.Parent = btn
            LloydUI:Bind(ico, "ImageColor3", "TextPrimary")
        end

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Font = FontBold
        lbl.TextSize = 11.5
        lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
        lbl.Text = langKey and T(langKey, fallbackText) or fallbackText
        lbl.LayoutOrder = 2
        lbl.Parent = btn
        LloydUI:Bind(lbl, "TextColor3", "TextPrimary")
        if langKey then
            LloydUI:RegisterText(lbl, langKey, nil, 11.5)
        end

        Motion.Press(btn, 0.96, 1.0)
        if not isMobile then
            btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                Tween(btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = tintCol and 0.58 or math.max(0.08, Theme.ButtonTrans - 0.12) })
                Tween(bStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 0.15 })
            end)
            btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                Tween(btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = tintCol and 0.76 or Theme.ButtonTrans })
                Tween(bStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = tintCol and 0.35 or Theme.ButtonBorderTrans })
            end)
        end
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            PlaySound(Sounds.Click, 0.75)
            if onClick then onClick(btn, lbl) end
        end)
        return btn, lbl
    end

    -- Captured Logs Feed Container
    local logsFeedContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    logsFeedContainer.Name = "CapturedLogsFeed"
    logsFeedContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    logsFeedContainer.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    logsFeedContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    logsFeedContainer.LayoutOrder = 2
    logsFeedContainer.Parent = bodyContainer

    lfLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
