local controlActive = false
local controlTargetName = nil
local controlClickConn = nil
local controlRefreshLoop = nil
local lastControlTpClock = 0

local function stopControl()
    if controlClickConn then pcall(function() controlClickConn:Disconnect() end); controlClickConn = nil end
    if controlRefreshLoop then pcall(function() task.cancel(controlRefreshLoop) end); controlRefreshLoop = nil end
    if controlTargetName and broadcastAdminCommand then
        pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("CONTROL_STOP", controlTargetName) end)
    end
    controlActive = false
    controlTargetName = nil
    local ac = LocalPlayer.Character
    local ah = ac and ac:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if Camera and ah then Camera.CameraSubject = ah end
end

local controlPlayerToggle = adminBox:AddToggle("tog_admin_control_player", "desc_admin_control_player", false, function(enabled)
    if not enabled then stopControl(); return end
    local curTarget = (adminTargetDrop and adminTargetDrop.Get and adminTargetDrop.Get()) or selectedAdminTarget or "all"
    if curTarget == "all" or curTarget == "" or not curTarget then
        LloydUI:Notify({ Title = "Control Player", Content = "Please select a specific target first (not All)", Icon = Icons.Close, Duration = 2.0 })
        if controlPlayerToggle then controlPlayerToggle.Set(false, true) end
        return
    end
    
    stopControl()
    controlActive = true
    controlTargetName = curTarget

    if broadcastAdminCommand then pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("CONTROL_START", curTarget) end) end

    local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(curTarget)
    local th = tp and tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if Camera and th then Camera.CameraSubject = th end

    -- ลูปคลิกพื้นเพื่อวาร์ปเป้าหมาย
    controlClickConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
        if gp then return end -- ถ้าคลิกที่ UI ไม่ต้องวาร์ป
        if not controlActive or not controlTargetName then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastControlTpClock < 0.25 then return end -- กันสแปม
        lastControlTpClock = now

        local mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
        if not mouse or not mouse.Hit then return end
        
        local pos = mouse.Hit.Position
        -- บวก 100000 เพื่อไม่ให้ติดลบ แล้วใช้ _ คั่น
        local px = math.floor(pos.X + 100000)
        local py = math.floor(pos.Y + 100000)
        local pz = math.floor(pos.Z + 100000)
        
        if broadcastAdminCommand then
            pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("CONTROL_TP_" .. px .. "_" .. py .. "_" .. pz, controlTargetName) end)
        end
    end)

    -- รีเฟรชสถานะควบคุมทุก 1.5 วิ
    controlRefreshLoop = task.spawn(function()
        while controlActive and controlTargetName do
            task.wait(1.5)
            if not controlActive then break end
            if broadcastAdminCommand then pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("CONTROL_START", controlTargetName) end) end
            local t = Players:FindFirstChild(controlTargetName)
            local h = t and t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if Camera and h then Camera.CameraSubject = h end
        end
    end)

    LloydUI:Notify({ Title = "Control Player", Content = "Now controlling: " .. curTarget .. " (Click world to teleport target)", Icon = Icons.Check, Duration = 3.0 })
end)

        -- Continuous Follow switch (ON = target keeps warping to admin until OFF)
        local adminFollowLoop = nil
        local adminFollowTarget = nil
        adminBox:AddToggle("tog_admin_follow", "desc_admin_follow", false, function(enabled)
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            if adminFollowLoop then
                pcall(function() task.cancel(adminFollowLoop) end)
                adminFollowLoop = nil
            end
            if not enabled then
                local stopTarget = adminFollowTarget
                    or (adminTargetDrop and adminTargetDrop.Get and adminTargetDrop.Get())
                    or selectedAdminTarget
                if stopTarget and stopTarget ~= "all" and broadcastAdminCommand then
                    pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("FOLLOW_STOP", stopTarget) end)
                end
                adminFollowTarget = nil
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Follow OFF" or "ปิดระบบตาม",
                    Content = isEn and "Target is no longer forced to follow you" or "เป้าหมายไม่ต้องตามเราแล้ว",
                    Icon = Icons.Check,
                    Duration = 1.8
                })
                return
            end

            local curTarget = (adminTargetDrop and adminTargetDrop.Get and adminTargetDrop.Get()) or selectedAdminTarget or "all"
            if curTarget == "all" or curTarget == "" or not curTarget then
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Select a Player" or "กรุณาเลือกผู้เล่น",
                    Content = isEn and "Pick a specific target first (not All)" or "ต้องเลือกผู้เล่นเป้าหมายก่อน (ห้ามใช้ All)",
                    Icon = Icons.Close,
                    Duration = 2.2
                })
                return
            end

            adminFollowTarget = curTarget
            selectedAdminTarget = curTarget
            local function pulseFollow()
                if not adminFollowTarget then return end
                pcall(function()
                    if broadcastAdminCommand then
                        broadcastAdminCommand("FOLLOW_START", adminFollowTarget)
                    end
                end)
            end
            pulseFollow()
            -- Re-broadcast every 2.5s so respawn / lag still re-engages follow
            adminFollowLoop = task.spawn(function()
                while adminFollowTarget do
                    task.wait(2.5)
                    if not adminFollowTarget then break end
                    pulseFollow()
                end
            end)
            LloydUI:Notify({
                Title = isEn and "Follow ON" or "เปิดระบบตาม",
                Content = (isEn and "Target will keep following you: @" or "เป้าหมายจะตามเราตลอด: @") .. tostring(curTarget),
                Icon = Icons.Check,
                Duration = 2.2
            })
        end)

        local adminFreezeLoop, adminFreezeTarget = nil, nil
        adminBox:AddToggle("tog_admin_freeze", "desc_admin_freeze", false, function(enabled)
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            if adminFreezeLoop then pcall(function() task.cancel(adminFreezeLoop) end); adminFreezeLoop = nil end
            if not enabled then
                local stopTarget = adminFreezeTarget or (adminTargetDrop and adminTargetDrop.Get and adminTargetDrop.Get()) or selectedAdminTarget
                if stopTarget and stopTarget ~= "all" and broadcastAdminCommand then
                    pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("FREEZE_STOP", stopTarget) end)
                end
                adminFreezeTarget = nil
                LloydUI:Notify({ Title = isEn and "Freeze OFF" or "ปิด Freeze", Content = isEn and "Unfrozen" or "ปลดแช่แข็งแล้ว", Icon = Icons.Check, Duration = 1.6 })
                return
            end
            local curTarget = (adminTargetDrop and adminTargetDrop.Get and adminTargetDrop.Get()) or selectedAdminTarget or "all"
            if curTarget == "all" or not curTarget or curTarget == "" then
                LloydUI:Notify({ Title = isEn and "Select a Player" or "กรุณาเลือกผู้เล่น", Content = isEn and "Pick a specific target (not All)" or "ต้องเลือกผู้เล่น (ห้าม All)", Icon = Icons.Close, Duration = 2.0 })
                return
            end
            adminFreezeTarget = curTarget
            selectedAdminTarget = curTarget
            local function pulse()
                if adminFreezeTarget and broadcastAdminCommand then
                    pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("FREEZE_START", adminFreezeTarget) end)
                end
            end
            pulse(); task.delay(0.15, pulse); task.delay(0.35, pulse)
            adminFreezeLoop = task.spawn(function()
                while adminFreezeTarget do
                    task.wait(0.8)
                    if not adminFreezeTarget then break end
                    pulse()
                end
            end)
            LloydUI:Notify({ Title = isEn and "Freeze ON" or "เปิด Freeze", Content = (isEn and "Frozen @" or "แช่แข็ง @") .. tostring(curTarget), Icon = Icons.Check, Duration = 2.0 })
        end)

        adminBox:AddButton("btn_admin_kick", function()
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            local curTarget = (adminTargetDrop and adminTargetDrop.Get and adminTargetDrop.Get()) or selectedAdminTarget or "all"
            if curTarget == "all" or not curTarget or curTarget == "" then
                LloydUI:Notify({ Title = isEn and "Select a Player" or "กรุณาเลือกผู้เล่น", Content = isEn and "Pick a specific target (not All)" or "ต้องเลือกผู้เล่น (ห้าม All)", Icon = Icons.Close, Duration = 2.0 })
                return
            end
            if broadcastAdminCommand then
                for _, d in ipairs({0, 0.12, 0.28, 0.5}) do
                    task.delay(d, function() pcall(function() broadcastAdminCommand("KICK", curTarget) end) end)
                end
            end
            LloydUI:Notify({ Title = isEn and "Kick Sent" or "ส่งคำสั่งเตะแล้ว", Content = (isEn and "Kick -> @" or "เตะ @") .. tostring(curTarget), Icon = Icons.Check, Duration = 2.0 })
        end)

        local function safeJoinServerInstance(targetPlaceId, targetJobId, displayLabel)
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            local ts = TeleportService or game:GetService("TeleportService")
            local cleanPlaceId = tonumber(targetPlaceId) or game.PlaceId
            local cleanJobId = string.gsub(tostring(targetJobId or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
            if cleanJobId == "" then
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Missing Server JobId" or "ไม่พบรหัสเซิร์ฟเวอร์ (JobId)",
                    Content = isEn and "Target server JobId is empty" or "ผู้เล่นนี้ไม่ได้อยู่ในเซิร์ฟเวอร์สาธารณะที่มี JobId",
                    Icon = Icons.Close,
                    Duration = 2.5
                })
                return false
            end

            pcall(function()
                if setclipboard then setclipboard(cleanJobId) end
            end)

            LloydUI:Notify({
                Title = isEn and "Joining Script User" or "กำลังจอยตามผู้ใช้สคริปต์",
                Content = string.format(
                    isEn and "Teleporting to %s [Server #%s]..." or "กำลังย้ายเซิร์ฟไปหา %s [เซิร์ฟ #%s]...",
                    tostring(displayLabel or "Target"), string.sub(cleanJobId, 1, 8)
                ),
                Icon = Icons.Navigation,
                Duration = 3.5
            })

            local failConn
            failConn = ts.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function(plr, teleportResult, errorMessage)
                if plr == LocalPlayer then
                    if failConn then failConn:Disconnect(); failConn = nil end
                    LloydUI:Notify({
                        Title = isEn and "Server Join Rejected" or "ไม่สามารถเข้าเซิร์ฟเวอร์เป้าหมายได้",
                        Content = string.format("%s (%s)", tostring(teleportResult and teleportResult.Name or teleportResult), tostring(errorMessage or "Server closed or full")),
                        Icon = Icons.Close,
                        Duration = 3.5
                    })
                end
            end)
            task.delay(10, function()
                if failConn then failConn:Disconnect(); failConn = nil end
            end)

            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                ts:TeleportToPlaceInstance(cleanPlaceId, cleanJobId, LocalPlayer)
            end)
            if not ok then
                if failConn then failConn:Disconnect(); failConn = nil end
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Teleport Failed" or "ไม่สามารถย้ายเซิร์ฟเวอร์ได้",
                    Content = tostring(err),
                    Icon = Icons.Close,
                    Duration = 3.2
                })
                return false
            end
            return true
        end

        adminBox:AddButton("btn_admin_goto", function()
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            local curTarget = (adminTargetDrop and adminTargetDrop.Get and adminTargetDrop.Get()) or selectedAdminTarget or "all"
            if curTarget == "all" then
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Admin Teleport" or "ระบบเทเลพอร์ตแอดมิน",
                    Content = isEn and "Please select a specific player first" or "กรุณาเลือกผู้เล่นเป้าหมายที่ต้องการวาร์ปไปหาก่อน",
                    Icon = Icons.Close,
                    Duration = 1.8
                })
                return
            end
            local tPlr = Players:FindFirstChild(curTarget)
            local tHrp = tPlr and tPlr.Character and tPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if tHrp and myHrp then
                myHrp.CFrame = tHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3.5)
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Admin Teleport" or "ระบบเทเลพอร์ตแอดมิน",
                    Content = (isEn and "Teleported to " or "เคลื่อนย้ายไปหา ") .. tPlr.DisplayName,
                    Icon = Icons.Check,
                    Duration = 1.8
                })
            else
                local cloudInfo = CloudScriptUsers[string.lower(curTarget)]
                local cJobId = cloudInfo and (cloudInfo.JobId or cloudInfo.jobId or "") or ""
                if cloudInfo and cJobId ~= "" and cJobId ~= game.JobId then
                    safeJoinServerInstance(cloudInfo.PlaceId or cloudInfo.placeId or game.PlaceId, cJobId, cloudInfo.DisplayName or cloudInfo.Name or curTarget)
                end
            end
        end)

        local function getCombinedScriptUsersMap()
            local map = {}
            local myLower = LocalPlayer and string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name) or ""
            -- 1. Cloud presence users (across all servers, excluding self)
            for lowerName, info in pairs(CloudScriptUsers) do
                local uName = info.Name or info.user or lowerName
                local uDisp = info.DisplayName or info.displayName or uName
                local lastTs = info.LastSeen or info.ts or 0
                local uJobId = info.JobId or info.jobId or ""
                local uPlaceId = info.PlaceId or info.placeId or game.PlaceId
                if lowerName ~= myLower and (os.time() - lastTs) <= 900 then
                    map[lowerName] = {
                        Name         = uName,
                        DisplayName  = uDisp,
                        UserId       = info.UserId or info.userId or 0,
                        PlaceId      = uPlaceId,
                        JobId        = uJobId,
                        LastSeen     = lastTs,
                        InSameServer = (uJobId ~= "" and uJobId == game.JobId) or (Players:FindFirstChild(uName) ~= nil),
                    }
                end
            end
            -- 2. In-server detected script users (excluding self)
            for plrName, ts in pairs(DetectedScriptUsers) do
                local lowerName = string.lower(plrName)
                if lowerName ~= myLower then
                    local p = Players:FindFirstChild(plrName)
                    if not map[lowerName] then
                        map[lowerName] = {
                            Name         = plrName,
                            DisplayName  = p and p.DisplayName or plrName,
                            UserId       = p and p.UserId or 0,
                            PlaceId      = game.PlaceId,
                            JobId        = game.JobId,
                            LastSeen     = math.floor(ts),
                            InSameServer = (p ~= nil),
                        }
                    else
                        if p then
                            map[lowerName].InSameServer = true
                            map[lowerName].JobId = game.JobId
                            map[lowerName].PlaceId = game.PlaceId
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return map
        end

        local function updateJoinerTelemetryCard()
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            local map = getCombinedScriptUsersMap()
            local totalUsers = 0
            local remoteUsers = 0
            for _, info in pairs(map) do
                totalUsers = totalUsers + 1
                if not info.InSameServer then
                    remoteUsers = remoteUsers + 1
                end
            end

            local chosen = (selectedJoinUserKey ~= "none") and map[string.lower(selectedJoinUserKey)] or nil
            if chosen then
                local stateTag = chosen.InSameServer
                    and (isEn and "In Current Server" or "อยู่ในเซิร์ฟเวอร์เดียวกัน")
                    or (isEn and "Online (Another Server)" or "ออนไลน์ (อยู่คนละเซิร์ฟเวอร์)")
                JoinStatusTitle.Text = string.format("%s (@%s)", chosen.DisplayName, chosen.Name)
                JoinStatusSub1.Text = string.format("%s  •  PlaceId: %s", stateTag, tostring(chosen.PlaceId))
                JoinStatusSub2.Text = "JobId: " .. ((chosen.JobId and chosen.JobId ~= "") and chosen.JobId or "N/A")
            else
                JoinStatusTitle.Text = isEn
                    and string.format("Verified Script Users: %d (%d Cross-Server)", totalUsers, remoteUsers)
                    or string.format("พบผู้ใช้สคริปต์เราออนไลน์: %d คน (ข้ามเซิร์ฟ %d คน)", totalUsers, remoteUsers)
                JoinStatusSub1.Text = isEn
                    and "Only players running our script are listed below"
                    or "แสดงเฉพาะรายชื่อผู้เล่นที่ตรวจพบว่าเปิดใช้สคริปต์เราเท่านั้น"
                JoinStatusSub2.Text = "Current JobId: " .. tostring(game.JobId ~= "" and game.JobId or "Local")
            end
        end

        local function getJoinerDropdownItems()
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            local map = getCombinedScriptUsersMap()
            local list = {}
            for lowerName, info in pairs(map) do
                table.insert(list, { Key = lowerName, Info = info })
            end
            table.sort(list, function(a, b)
                if a.Info.InSameServer ~= b.Info.InSameServer then
                    return not a.Info.InSameServer -- Put cross-server users first for joining
                end
                return tostring(a.Info.Name) < tostring(b.Info.Name)
            end)

            local items = {}
            if #list == 0 then
                table.insert(items, {
                    Value = "none",
                    Label = isEn and "No other script users detected yet" or "ยังไม่มีผู้เล่นคนอื่นที่ใช้สคริปต์เราในขณะนี้"
                })
            else
                for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                    local info = entry.Info
                    local serverTag = info.InSameServer
                        and (isEn and " [Same Server]" or " [เซิร์ฟเดียวกัน]")
                        or (isEn and " [Joinable Server]" or " [คนละเซิร์ฟ - จอยได้]")
                    local shortJob = (info.JobId and #info.JobId >= 8) and string.sub(info.JobId, 1, 8) or "local"
                    table.insert(items, {
                        Value = entry.Key,
                        Label = string.format("%s (@%s)%s • #%s", info.DisplayName, info.Name, serverTag, shortJob)
                    })
                end
            end
            return items
        end

        local joinTargetDrop = joinerBox:AddDropdown("drop_admin_join_target", getJoinerDropdownItems(), "none", function(val)
            selectedJoinUserKey = val or "none"
            updateJoinerTelemetryCard()
        end)

        local function refreshAdminJoinerUI()
            local items = getJoinerDropdownItems()
            if (selectedJoinUserKey == "none" or not getCombinedScriptUsersMap()[string.lower(selectedJoinUserKey)]) and items[1] then
                selectedJoinUserKey = items[1].Value
            end
            joinTargetDrop.Refresh(items, selectedJoinUserKey)
            adminTargetDrop.Refresh(getAdminTargetDropdownItems(), selectedAdminTarget)
            updateJoinerTelemetryCard()
        end
        _G.LloydRefreshAdminJoiner = refreshAdminJoinerUI

        joinerBox:AddButton("btn_admin_refresh_cloud", function()
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            LloydUI:Notify({
                Title = isEn and "Scanning Script Users" or "กำลังค้นหาผู้ใช้สคริปต์",
                Content = isEn and "Polling cross-server presence bus..." or "กำลังดึงรายชื่อผู้ที่เปิดใช้สคริปต์เราจากทุกเซิร์ฟเวอร์...",
                Icon = Icons.Refresh,
                Duration = 1.6
            })
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function() publishCloudPresence("SCAN") end)
                pcall(function()
                    pollCloudBus("20m", function()
                        refreshAdminJoinerUI()
                        local map = getCombinedScriptUsersMap()
                        local count = 0
                        for _ in pairs(map) do count = count + 1 end
                        LloydUI:Notify({
                            Title = isEn and "Scan Complete" or "อัปเดตรายชื่อสำเร็จ",
                            Content = isEn
                                and string.format("Found %d verified script user(s) online", count)
                                or string.format("ตรวจพบผู้เล่นที่ใช้สคริปต์เราออนไลน์ทั้งหมด %d คน", count),
                            Icon = Icons.Check,
                            Duration = 2.2
                        })
                    end, true)
                end)
            end)
        end)

        joinerBox:AddButton("btn_admin_join_server", function()
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            local map = getCombinedScriptUsersMap()
            local rawKey = (joinTargetDrop and joinTargetDrop.Get and joinTargetDrop.Get()) or selectedJoinUserKey or "none"
            local chosen = (rawKey ~= "none") and map[string.lower(tostring(rawKey))] or nil
            if not chosen then
                -- Fallback: pick the first available user in the dropdown list if one exists
                local fallbackItems = getJoinerDropdownItems()
                if fallbackItems[1] and fallbackItems[1].Value ~= "none" then
                    selectedJoinUserKey = fallbackItems[1].Value
                    chosen = map[string.lower(tostring(selectedJoinUserKey))]
                end
            end
            if not chosen then
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Select Script User" or "กรุณาเลือกผู้เล่น",
                    Content = isEn and "Please select an active script user from the list first" or "กรุณาเลือกรายชื่อผู้ใช้สคริปต์ที่ต้องการจอยตามก่อน",
                    Icon = Icons.Close,
                    Duration = 2.0
                })
                return
            end

            if chosen.JobId and chosen.JobId ~= "" and chosen.JobId ~= game.JobId then
                safeJoinServerInstance(chosen.PlaceId or game.PlaceId, chosen.JobId, string.format("%s (@%s)", chosen.DisplayName, chosen.Name))
            else
                -- Already in the same server -> teleport character directly to them!
                local tPlr = Players:FindFirstChild(chosen.Name)
                local tHrp = tPlr and tPlr.Character and tPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if tHrp and myHrp then
                    myHrp.CFrame = tHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3.5)
                    LloydUI:Notify({
                        Title = isEn and "Same Server Teleport" or "อยู่ในเซิร์ฟเวอร์เดียวกันแล้ว",
                        Content = (isEn and "Teleported directly to " or "วาร์ปไปหาตัวละครของ ") .. chosen.DisplayName,
                        Icon = Icons.Check,
                        Duration = 2.2
                    })
                elseif chosen.JobId and chosen.JobId ~= "" then
                    safeJoinServerInstance(chosen.PlaceId or game.PlaceId, chosen.JobId, string.format("%s (@%s)", chosen.DisplayName, chosen.Name))
                end
            end
        end)

        local manualJoinInput = joinerBox:AddInput("inp_admin_manual_join", "ph_admin_manual_join", "", function(txt)
            manualJoinQuery = string.gsub(tostring(txt or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
        end)

        joinerBox:AddButton("btn_admin_manual_join", function()
            local isEn = (AppState.LangMode == "en")
            local rawTxt = (manualJoinInput and manualJoinInput.Get and manualJoinInput.Get()) or manualJoinQuery or ""
            local q = string.gsub(tostring(rawTxt), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
            if q == "" then
                LloydUI:Notify({
                    Title = isEn and "Input Required" or "กรุณาระบุข้อมูล",
                    Content = isEn and "Enter a Roblox Username or Server JobId first" or "กรุณาพิมพ์ชื่อผู้เล่น (Username) หรือรหัส JobId ก่อนกดจอย",
                    Icon = Icons.Close,
                    Duration = 2.0
                })
                return
            end

            -- Case 1: Input is a Server JobId UUID (e.g. 312a6516-b1da-4814-8557-1f3eb1388401)
            if string.match(q, "^[%x]+%-[%x]+%-[%x]+%-[%x]+%-[%x]+$") then
                safeJoinServerInstance(game.PlaceId, q, "JobId")
                return
            end

            -- Case 2: Check CloudScriptUsers by username or display name
            local lowerQ = string.lower(q)
            local map = getCombinedScriptUsersMap()
            local matchedInfo = map[lowerQ]
            if not matchedInfo then
                for _, info in pairs(map) do
                    if string.find(string.lower(info.Name), lowerQ, 1, true) or string.find(string.lower(info.DisplayName or ""), lowerQ, 1, true) then
                        matchedInfo = info
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if matchedInfo and matchedInfo.JobId and matchedInfo.JobId ~= "" then
                if matchedInfo.JobId ~= game.JobId then
                    safeJoinServerInstance(matchedInfo.PlaceId or game.PlaceId, matchedInfo.JobId, string.format("%s (@%s)", matchedInfo.DisplayName, matchedInfo.Name))
                else
                    local tPlr = Players:FindFirstChild(matchedInfo.Name)
                    local tHrp = tPlr and tPlr.Character and tPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if tHrp and myHrp then
                        myHrp.CFrame = tHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3.5)
                    end
                    LloydUI:Notify({
                        Title = isEn and "Same Server" or "อยู่ในเซิร์ฟเวอร์เดียวกัน",
                        Content = (isEn and "Teleported directly to " or "วาร์ปไปหา ") .. matchedInfo.Name,
                        Icon = Icons.Check,
                        Duration = 2.2
                    })
                end
            else
                -- Check if in current server even without script
                local inServerPlr = nil
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if string.lower(p.Name) == lowerQ or string.find(string.lower(p.Name), lowerQ, 1, true) then
                        inServerPlr = p
                        break
                    end
                end
                if inServerPlr and inServerPlr.Character and inServerPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if myHrp then
                        myHrp.CFrame = inServerPlr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3.5)
                        LloydUI:Notify({
                            Title = isEn and "Teleported in Server" or "วาร์ปไปหาผู้เล่นสำเร็จ",
                            Content = inServerPlr.DisplayName .. " (@" .. inServerPlr.Name .. ")",
                            Icon = Icons.Check,
                            Duration = 2.0
                        })
                    end
                else
                    LloydUI:Notify({
                        Title = isEn and "User Not Found on Script Radar" or "ยังไม่พบผู้เล่นนี้ในระบบสคริปต์",
                        Content = isEn
                            and "Target is not currently broadcasting on our script or JobId was not provided"
                            or "ผู้เล่นนี้ยังไม่ได้เปิดใช้สคริปต์ของเราในขณะนี้ หรือกรุณาระบุ JobId โดยตรง",
                        Icon = Icons.Close,
                        Duration = 2.8
                    })
                end
            end
        end)

        table.insert(LloydUI.LangCallbacks, function()
            refreshAdminJoinerUI()
        end)

        task.defer(refreshAdminJoinerUI)
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  KFC SENTINEL v2.0 — Anti-Target-Only (เตะเฉพาะตอนโดนดัก)
--  วางหลัง LloydUI:Notify loaded block ก่อน print
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local KFC_KICK_MSG = "🛡️ อย่าดักซอร์สผมเลยPLS ใช้ให้สนุกครับ"
local Sentinel = {}
local kicked = false

local function doKick(reason)
    if kicked then return end
    kicked = true
    warn("[KFC Sentinel] ⛔ Kicked: " .. tostring(reason))
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:Kick(KFC_KICK_MSG)
    end)
    task.delay(0.2, function()
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:Kick(KFC_KICK_MSG)
        end)
    end)
end

-- ═══════════════════ 1. เก็บ reference ฟังก์ชันสำคัญของเรา ═══════════════════
-- เราจะเช็คว่าถ้ามีคนเรียก hook เหล่านี้กับ "ฟังก์ชันของเรา" = พยายามดัก
local OUR_FUNCTIONS = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- weak keys
local function markAsOurs(fn)
    if type(fn) == "function" then
        OUR_FUNCTIONS[fn] = true
    end
    return fn
end

local function isOurs(fn)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return false end
    return OUR_FUNCTIONS[fn] == true
end

-- mark ฟังก์ชันหลักของเราเป็น "ของเรา"
markAsOurs(LloydUI.Notify)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.SwitchToPage)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.SetIslandMode)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.CreateWindow)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.CreateDock)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.CreateNowPlayingWidget)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.UpdateAllTexts)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.ApplyTheme)
markAsOurs(LloydUI.SetAccentColor)
markAsOurs(Motion.Tween)
markAsOurs(Motion.Press)
markAsOurs(PlaySound)
markAsOurs(PlayChime)
if _G.LloydPlayMusicFn then markAsOurs(_G.LloydPlayMusicFn) end
if _G.LloydStopMusicFn then markAsOurs(_G.LloydStopMusicFn) end
if LloydUI.BroadcastAdminCommand then markAsOurs(LloydUI.BroadcastAdminCommand) end
if LloydUI.PublishCloudPresence then markAsOurs(LloydUI.PublishCloudPresence) end

-- ═══════════════════ 2. WRAP string.dump — จับการดักฟังก์ชันของเรา ═══════════════════
if type(string.dump) == "function" then
    local original_dump = string.dump
    string.dump = function(fn, strip)
        if isOurs(fn) then
            doKick("string.dump(targeted our function)")
            return ""
        end
        return original_dump(fn, strip)
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════ 3. WRAP debug.getinfo / getupvalue / getlocal ═══════════════════
if debug then
    if type(debug.getinfo) == "function" then
        local orig = debug.getinfo
        debug.getinfo = function(fn, ...)
            if isOurs(fn) then
                doKick("debug.getinfo(targeted our function)")
                return nil
            end
            return orig(fn, ...)
        end
    end

    if type(debug.getupvalue) == "function" then
        local orig = debug.getupvalue
        debug.getupvalue = function(fn, idx)
            if isOurs(fn) then
                doKick("debug.getupvalue(targeted our function)")
                return nil, nil
            end
            return orig(fn, idx)
        end
    end

    if type(debug.getlocal) == "function" then
        local orig = debug.getlocal
        debug.getlocal = function(fn, ...)
            -- getlocal(level) รับ number ไม่ใช่ function → ปล่อยผ่าน
            -- getlocal(function, index) = จับ
            if type(fn) == "function" and isOurs(fn) then
                doKick("debug.getlocal(targeted our function)")
                return nil, nil
            end
            return orig(fn, ...)
        end
    end

    if type(debug.setupvalue) == "function" then
        local orig = debug.setupvalue
        debug.setupvalue = function(fn, idx, val)
            if isOurs(fn) then
                doKick("debug.setupvalue(targeted our function)")
                return nil
            end
            return orig(fn, idx, val)
        end
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════ 4. WRAP getscriptbytecode / getscriptclosure / getscripthash ═══════════════════
-- Executor บางตัวมีฟังก์ชันนี้ ใช้ดึง bytecode ของสคริปต์โดยตรง
local function wrapExecutorFn(globalName, hookName)
    local original = _G[globalName]
    if type(original) ~= "function" then return end
    _G[globalName] = function(target, ...)
        -- ถ้าดึง script ทั้งหมด หรือสคริปต์ของเรา
        if target == nil then
            -- getscriptbytecode() ไม่มี arg = กำลังดึงสคริปต์ปัจจุบันของเรา!
            doKick(hookName .. "() with no target (dumping current script)")
            return ""
        end
        -- ถ้า target เป็น script instance ที่รันสคริปต์ของเรา
        if typeof and typeof(target) == "Instance" then
            local ok, isOurs = pcall(function()
                local env = getfenv(0)
                local myScript = env.script
                return target == myScript or target:IsDescendantOf(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts"))
            end)
            if ok and isOurs then
                doKick(hookName .. "(targeted our script)")
                return ""
            end
        end
        return original(target, ...)
    end
end

wrapExecutorFn("getscriptbytecode", "getscriptbytecode")
wrapExecutorFn("getscriptclosure", "getscriptclosure")
wrapExecutorFn("getscripthash", "getscripthash")
wrapExecutorFn("getscriptfromstring", "getscriptfromstring")
wrapExecutorFn("dumpstring", "dumpstring")

-- ═══════════════════ 5. WRAP loadstring — บล็อกการโหลดซอร์สที่ผิดปกติ ═══════════════════
-- แต่ไม่บล็อกการโหลดปกติ (แค่เตือน + เตะถ้าโหลดซอร์สที่ดูเหมือนจะ hook เรา)
local original_loadstring = loadstring
if type(original_loadstring) == "function" then
    _G.loadstring = function(code, chunkname, ...)
        if type(code) == "string" then
            -- ถ้าโค้ดพยายาม getscriptbytecode/string.dump/debug.getinfo กับตัวแปร "_G" หรือ "LloydUI"
            -- ให้ผ่าน (เพราะอาจเป็น executor ปกติ)
            -- แต่ถ้าโค้ดเรียกกับ "ฟังก์ชันของเรา" ตรงๆ → เตะ
            if #code > 50 and (
                string.find(code, "string%.dump%s*%(%s*LloydUI") or
                string.find(code, "debug%.getinfo%s*%(%s*LloydUI") or
                string.find(code, "getscriptbytecode")
            ) then
                -- มีโอกาสสูงที่เป็น spy/logger → เตะ
                doKick("loadstring containing dump/inspect on our stuff")
                return nil, "Blocked"
            end
        end
        return original_loadstring(code, chunkname, ...)
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════ 6. ตรวจจับ metatable tampering ═══════════════════
-- ถ้ามีคนเปลี่ยน metatable ของ string/debug/table เพื่อ hook เรา
local stringMt = getmetatable("")
local debugMt = getmetatable(debug)
local originalStringIndex = stringMt and rawget(stringMt, "__index")
local originalDebugIndex = debugMt and rawget(debugMt, "__index")

local function checkMetatables()
    if stringMt and originalStringIndex then
        local cur = rawget(stringMt, "__index")
        if cur ~= originalStringIndex then
            doKick("string metatable tampered (hook attempt)")
            return
        end
    end
    if debugMt and originalDebugIndex then
        local cur = rawget(debugMt, "__index")
        if cur ~= originalDebugIndex then
            doKick("debug metatable tampered (hook attempt)")
            return
        end
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════ 7. ตรวจว่าฟังก์ชันของเราถูกแทนที่หรือไม่ ═══════════════════
-- เก็บ hash ของ tostring(ฟังก์ชัน) ไว้เทียบ
local functionRefHashes = {}
local function captureFuncRefs()
    for fn, _ in pairs(OUR_FUNCTIONS) do
        functionRefHashes[tostring(fn)] = true
    end
end
captureFuncRefs()

local function checkFunctionRefsIntact()
    -- ถ้าฟังก์ชันใดถูกแทนที่ = tostring จะเปลี่ยน → error
    for fn, _ in pairs(OUR_FUNCTIONS) do
        local ok, str = pcall(tostring, fn)
        if not ok or type(str) ~= "string" then
            doKick("function reference destroyed")
            return
        end
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════ 8. ตรวจ getgc / getreg ที่เรียกซ้ำๆ ═══════════════════
-- ถ้ามีคนสแกน GC ถี่ๆ ในขณะที่สคริปต์เราทำงาน = น่าจะกำลังหาฟังก์ชันเรา
local getgcCount = 0
local lastGetgcTime = 0
local function wrapScanner(name)
    local orig = _G[name]
    if type(orig) ~= "function" then return end
    _G[name] = function(...)
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastGetgcTime > 1.5 then
            getgcCount = 0
        end
        lastGetgcTime = now
        getgcCount = getgcCount + 1
        -- ถ้าเรียกเกิน 15 ครั้งใน 1.5 วิ = สแกนหาเราอยู่ → เตะ
        if getgcCount > 15 then
            doKick(name .. "() called rapidly (GC scanning for our functions)")
            return {}
        end
        return orig(...)
    end
end
wrapScanner("getgc")
wrapScanner("getreg")

-- ═══════════════════ 9. ตรวจ getloadedmodules — ดึงรายการ module ═══════════════════
local getloadedmodulesOriginal = _G.getloadedmodules
if type(getloadedmodulesOriginal) == "function" then
    _G.getloadedmodules = function(...)
        -- ปล่อยผ่าน (ปกติใช้ legit) แต่ log ไว้
        -- ถ้าเรียกถี่ๆ (ใน 0.5 วิ เกิน 5 ครั้ง) = สแกนหาเรา
        local now = os.clock()
        _G._kfc_lastglm = _G._kfc_lastglm or 0
        _G._kfc_glmcount = _G._kfc_glmcount or 0
        if now - _G._kfc_lastglm > 0.5 then
            _G._kfc_glmcount = 0
        end
        _G._kfc_lastglm = now
        _G._kfc_glmcount = _G._kfc_glmcount + 1
        if _G._kfc_glmcount > 8 then
            doKick("getloadedmodules() spam (module scanning)")
            return {}
        end
        return getloadedmodulesOriginal(...)
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════ 10. PERIODIC CHECK ═══════════════════
local checkAccum = 0
local integrityConn
integrityConn = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    if kicked then
        pcall(function() integrityConn:Disconnect() end)
        return
    end
    checkAccum = checkAccum + dt
    if checkAccum < 3.5 then return end
    checkAccum = 0
    pcall(checkMetatables)
    pcall(checkFunctionRefsIntact)
end)

-- ═══════════════════ 11. EXPORT API ═══════════════════
Sentinel.doKick = doKick
Sentinel.markAsOurs = markAsOurs
Sentinel.isOurs = isOurs
_G.KFC_Sentinel = Sentinel

-- 🛠️ SCOOTER RIDE FIX v2 (fake-seat + auto animation)
(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local SCOOTER_NAMES = { BlueScooter = true, PinkScooter = true }
    local activeTrack, activeModel = nil, nil

    local function findAnim(name)
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Animation") and d.Name == name then return d end
            end
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(RS:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("Animation") and d.Name == name then return d end
        end
    end

    local function stopRide()
        if activeTrack then pcall(function() activeTrack:Stop(0) end); activeTrack = nil end
        activeModel = nil
    end

    local function playRide(model)
        if not model or not model.Parent then return end
        stopRide()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then animator = Instance.new("Animator"); animator.Parent = hum end
        activeModel = model
        local anim = findAnim("ScooterAnimation") or findAnim("RiderAnimationOne")
        if not anim then warn("[KFC] ไม่พบ animation"); return end
        local track = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
        track.Looped = true
        track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
        track:Play(0.1)
        activeTrack = track
        local ac
        ac = model.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
            if not model.Parent and model == activeModel then
                stopRide(); if ac then ac:Disconnect() end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function mountPlayer(model)
        if model:FindFirstChild("KFC_MountSeat") then return end
        local middle = model:FindFirstChild("Middle")
        if not middle then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not hrp then return end
        hrp.CFrame = middle.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2.5, 0)
        task.wait(0.05)
        local seat = Instance.new("VehicleSeat")
        seat.Name = "KFC_MountSeat"
        seat.Size = Vector3.new(2, 0.4, 2)
        seat.Transparency = 1
        seat.CanCollide = false
        seat.Anchored = false
        seat.CFrame = middle.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1.2, 0)
        seat.Parent = model
        local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
        w.Part0 = seat; w.Part1 = middle; w.Parent = seat
        task.wait(0.1)
        pcall(function() seat:Sit(hum) end)
    end

    local function handleScooter(model)
        if not model or not model.Parent then return end
        task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.15); mountPlayer(model) end)
        task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.25); playRide(model) end)
    end

    local function hookChar(char)
        if not char then return end
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "NoMotorVehicleModel" then
                local vn = child:GetAttribute("vehicleName")
                if SCOOTER_NAMES[vn] then handleScooter(child) end
            end
        end)
        char.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "NoMotorVehicleModel" and child == activeModel then stopRide() end
        end)
        local ex = char:FindFirstChild("NoMotorVehicleModel")
        if ex then
            local vn = ex:GetAttribute("vehicleName")
            if SCOOTER_NAMES[vn] then handleScooter(ex) end
        end
    end

    hookChar(LP.Character)
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookChar)
    _G.KFC_StopRide = stopRide
    _G.KFC_PlayRide = playRide
    _G.KFC_HandleScooter = handleScooter
    print("[KFC Scooter Ride] Fix v2 ready")
end)()

print("[Edition v7.0 by kfc_punyai] credits i5zzs(191) / kiru_66(HONKUKI) / pao.no1.(Pao)")
